120. 【2611.14818】What Do Tokenizer Comparisons Measure? Disentangling Granularity, Root Assignment, and Word-Level Readout across Amharic and Hausa

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14818

作者：Abdul Rehman Antall,Baidya Nath Saha

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：morphologically rich languages, Morphology-aware tokenizers, byte-pair encoding, morphologically rich, BPE

备注： 19 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Morphology-aware tokenizers are expected to help morphologically rich languages, and their reported gains over byte-pair encoding (BPE) are usually credited to their linguistic units. A comparison with BPE cannot support this attribution, since such a tokenizer also segments text more finely (granularity) and, on token-level tasks, changes how much the sub-token readout matters. We separate both factors from the effect of grouping words by their roots (root assignment) in Amharic, a Semitic language whose Ge'ez abugida hides consonantal roots inside syllabic glyphs, with Latin-script Hausa as an orthographic contrast. Our design combines a lossless root-and-pattern tokenizer with controls that change one factor at a time, in two independent implementations. In both, real root assignment improves Amharic named-entity recognition over scrambled roots (+4.9 and +4.7 F1), and root-frequency structure explains little of this gain. In small from-scratch models, the readout choice moves F1 by up to 13.4 points and reverses the sign of every comparison of the root-and-pattern arms with BPE, whereas in two pretrained encoders it moves F1 by less than one point. The granularity effect changes sign across languages and implementations. We recommend decomposing tokenizer effects, and reporting the readout, before a gain is attributed to linguistic structure.

121. 【2611.14801】Distributionally Robust BEST-RQ For Speech Representation Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14801

作者：Muhammad Khan,Xiaodong Cui,Brian Kingsbury,George Saon

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS); Optimization and Control (math.OC)

关键词：well-established self-supervised learning, large-scale unlabeled audio, unlabeled audio due, learn speech representations, self-supervised learning approach

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:BEST-RQ, based on random projection and quantization, has been a well-established self-supervised learning approach to learn speech representations from large-scale unlabeled audio due to its high performance and efficiency. However, in BEST-RQ representations learned by random quantization can be sensitive to the choice of quantization targets. To address this issue, we propose a distributionally robust extension of BEST-RQ that seeks representations robust across multiple quantization configurations. Specifically, we define groups by codebooks and formulate the training as a group Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) problem. Rather than optimizing solely for the average loss across groups, our approach adaptively reweights groups to emphasize those performing poorly. Additionally, we regularize DRO with a Kullback-Leibler divergence to prevent excessive distribution deviation. We evaluate our approach on the Multilingual LibriSpeech dataset and show that the distributionally robust BEST-RQ achieves superior performance over the conventional uniform average across multiple codebooks.

122. 【2611.14794】When Is Proof-Step Verification Worth Its Cost? Operating Requirements of Certify-or-Decline Verification for Open-Weight Mathematical Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14794

作者：Abhinav Gupta,Ben Slivinski,Yashica Patodia,Michael Saldivar

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Proof-step verification rewrites, Proof-step verification, certify or decline, verification rewrites, rewrites a solver

备注： 13 pages, 1 figure, 13 tables. Accepted at the 6th Workshop on Mathematical Reasoning and AI (MATH-AI) at NeurIPS 2026. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/yashica-patodia/certify-or-decline)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Proof-step verification rewrites a solver's answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier's pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. Open-weight auditor pairs show near-zero chance-corrected agreement, which measures consistency rather than accuracy against proof truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. We provide per-configuration results and evaluation details in the supplement.

123. 【2611.14774】An LLM Is All You Need: Data-Efficient Adaptation to Full-Duplex, Word-Level Spoken Dialogue

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14774

作者：Tairan Chen,Richard Yucheng He,Yihang Liu,Baodong Cao,Chen Xu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Sound (cs.SD); Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS)

关键词：Full-duplex spoken dialogue, spoken dialogue, hundreds of thousands, thousands to millions, millions of hours

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Full-duplex spoken dialogue, in which a system listens while it speaks and can backchannel, yield the floor, and be interrupted, is usually obtained by training on hundreds of thousands to millions of hours of speech. This is costly, rewrites the language model that supplies the system's knowledge, and still leaves turn-taking either implicit or decided coarsely rather than word by word. We show that a frozen, general-purpose multimodal LLM can instead be adapted into a word-level full-duplex system with little data. Our system is a thinker-talker cascade on an 80 ms frame clock. The thinker keeps the LLM frozen and trains only lightweight adapters and a few new token embeddings (97M parameters); at every frame it emits one token of agent text, with pauses, yields, and onsets represented as tokens of the same vocabulary and kept legal by a finite-state machine. A four-stage curriculum, each stage anchored to the model's own behavior under the previous format, performs the conversion in about 30 GPU-hours. The talker reads only the thinker's discrete tokens, so the two halves are trained independently. On Full-Duplex-Bench and InteractionBench, the system is competitive with open-source full-duplex systems built through large-scale speech training: among them, it is the least likely to take the floor when the user merely pauses, backchannels most often, and leads in spoken-task accuracy, groundedness, and coherence over long conversations.

124. 【2611.14766】Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14766

作者：Xiulin Yang,Sirisha Gudavalli,Jennifer Hu

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：humans, temporal cues, Abstract, social, others' mental states

备注： 30 pages, 16 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14758

备注： 18 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform strongly on multimodal tasks, Document Understanding (DU) among them, but the benchmarks that measure them mostly cover English documents and narrow problems. How well current VLMs generalize across languages, tasks, styles and layouts therefore remains unclear. We introduce MDU, a multilingual DU benchmark of 23146 examples spanning 13 tasks and five languages: English, French, German, Italian, and Spanish. The tasks isolate complementary capabilities, from surface-level text and Markdown extraction through summarization, classification and named-entity recognition to layout-sensitive passage and layout extraction. We evaluate 45 state-of-the-art open-weight VLMs from 1.7B to 235B parameters, plus one commercial system under the same protocol. The benchmark is far from saturated: the best average is 69.0, only eight of the 45 evaluated systems reach 65, and the two best results come from 27B models rather than from the largest systems we evaluate. Performance is uneven in ways that scale does not fix. The gap between the strongest and weakest language does not close as models grow, and it survives controlling for the domain composition of each language. Capabilities also do not improve together: once we partial out overall model quality, per-task scores are largely uncorrelated, and transcription ability in particular trades off against semantic extraction.

126. 【2611.14704】Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14704

作者：Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (stat.ML)

关键词：increasingly make consequential, problems alongside humans, make consequential decisions, solve problems alongside, large language models

备注： 30 pages, 11 figures, 3 tables, The Fortieth Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at this https URL.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14700

作者：Julie Zhu,Jianheng Hou,Zhixu Silvia Tao,Shi Bo,Yilan Fan,Yixuan He,Zhiyuan Hu,Shirley Huang,Fangyu Liu,Yijun Wang,Zibu Wei,Haimingyue Xu,Zhen Xu,Zichen Yuan,Jierui Zuo,Jintao Huang,Yifan Wang,Qianfeng Wen,Jiahan Li,Zhejian Peng,Bowen Yang,Shushu Liang,Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:User research and system testing reveal whether products and services meet user needs, yet recruiting specialized participants, designing controlled what-if comparisons, and repeating studies as designs evolve often bottleneck design iteration and data-driven decision-making. We present a framework and execution infrastructure that translates real-world industry inquiries into controlled experiments with simulated users. Our methodology formally defines the target cohort, experimental stimuli, and decision-relevant metrics, while the pipeline supports large-scale concurrent runs over cohort-stimulus-environment combinations (survey, web, chatbot, and OS app), auditable trial-level logging, cohort-level outcome aggregation, and cross-cohort and cross-variant statistical reports. Together, these studies show that persona-enabled agents can test specified cohorts at design speed, diagnose systems under test, and deliver directional evidence that focuses problem definition, steers design and strategic decisions, and guides further study.

128. 【2611.14683】rait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14683

作者：Adil Soubki,Elizabeth S.Z. Tan,Miles Cranmer

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Reinforcement learning, growing rapidly, standard stage, compute devoted, Reinforcement

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

129. 【2611.14675】MITRA-Swarm: Injecting Explicit Linguistic Structure into Foundation Models for Classical Machine Translation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14675

作者：Kayshav Bhardwaj,Pragun Seth,Kush Bhardwaj,Vijay Nagasamy,Kurt Keutzer

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Classical Sanskrit poses, morphophonemic fusion Sandhi, nominal compounding Samāsa, Classical Sanskrit, dense nominal compounding

备注： 17 pages, accepted to NeurIPS 2026 LP4FM Workshop

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Classical Sanskrit poses three challenges unresolved by single-call monolithic models: morphophonemic fusion Sandhi, dense nominal compounding Samāsa, and discourse-level zero-anaphora forced by poetic meter. We present MITRA-Swarm, a multi-agent architecture injecting explicit linguistic structure into an otherwise monolithic pipeline: a Sandhi-splitting agent, a parallel array of generalist gemma-4-26B-A4B-it and domain-specialized gemma-2-mitra-it translators, and a discourse-aware Ellipsis Restorer that consults corpus-derived register tags to avoid inventing active-voice subjects in impersonal legal and philosophical registers. Two separated judges evaluate quality: a routing judge ($J_{\text{route}}$, same model family as the generators) driving an internal critique loop, and an independent reporting judge ($J_{\text{report}}=$gpt-4o-mini, no shared vendor lineage) scoring every output exactly once. On a stratified benchmark of 1,000 verses across five registers (23,990 scored verdicts, 12-condition pre-registered ablation), the full swarm reaches $85.90$ MQM against a monolithic baseline of $85.27$ ($\Delta=+0.63$, $p_{\text{adj}}=0.0018$, 95% CI $[+0.30,+0.97]$), and isolated discourse restoration alone accounts for $+0.44$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$), outperforming a call-count-matched self-refinement baseline by $+0.51$ MQM ($p_{\text{adj}}=0.0288$). Severe per-verse failures (MQM$80$) fall from $9.0\%$ to $7.6\%$, at roughly $9\times$ the per-verse inference cost of the baseline. Additionally, $J_{\text{route}}$ scores its own family's outputs $2$-$7$ MQM higher than $J_{\text{report}}$ on every one of the 12 conditions, empirically motivating the two-judge split; and a preflight screen of a 192,151-entry retrieval index against all 12 evaluation works verifies 0 reference leaks across 269 runtime checks. These are small gains on a metric saturating near 85-86.

130. 【2611.14661】Abstention Is Not Threshold Compliance: Stated Scoring Rules in Open Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14661

作者：Dongsun Moon

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：propose stating, Kalai, abstention, abstention format, format

备注： 34 pages, 3 figures, 29 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Kalai et al. propose stating the scoring rule in the prompt with an explicit threshold t, so that a model should answer only when its probability of being correct exceeds t. We probe each requirement of compliance on shared items, in 12 checkpoints from three post-training stages of four fully open 3-8B recipes, with exploratory models up to 32B. At t = 0.75, a line limiting the reply to an answer or "I abstain" (the abstention format) raised the mean abstention rate from 20% to 91%. Yet within this format, raising t from 0.5 to 0.9 changed it by only -0.8 percentage points. Without the line, the higher threshold even lowered abstention, and up to 40% of a checkpoint's items received decisions that no compliant policy with threshold-independent beliefs could make. Given the probability that their answer was correct, the checkpoints barely responded to the threshold in a forced-choice probe. Comparisons with self-assessment signals such as P(True) depended on the grader and on answer drift between prompts. An external threshold on recalibrated P(True) made coverage non-increasing in t by construction. Calibrated on the evaluation distribution (post hoc), it beat the abstention format at t = 0.5; calibrated on TriviaQA and applied to PopQA, however, it fell below that format at 0.75. On OLMo 3 7B, decisions trained on threshold-conditioned targets did not follow unseen thresholds consistently. A post-hoc variant trained at four thresholds answered less as t rose but still failed a pre-specified test, mainly on agreement with its training rule. A high abstention rate is not evidence of compliance: evaluations using stated scoring rules should report how often models abstain, whether decisions respond to the stated threshold, and which items they answer.

131. 【2611.14654】Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14654

作者：Egor Pakhomov,Anurag Koul,Hongjun Liu,Bo Pang,Shafiq Joty,Erik Nijkamp

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Tool-using language-model agents, Tool-using language-model, language-model agents accumulate, agents accumulate histories, world

备注： 23 pages, 6 figures; code and data in ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.

133. 【2611.17035】Prevalence Bounds for Edited Watermarked Text: Power Envelopes and Calibration Sensitivity

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17035

作者：Kuancheng Ye

类目：Machine Learning (stat.ML); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Watermark detectors decide, watermarked language model, language model, detectors decide, Watermark detectors

备注： 31 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables. Code and results: [this https URL](https://github.com/YYKKCC-kevin/watermark-prevalence)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Watermark detectors decide whether one document came from a watermarked language model. Auditors often need a different number: the share of a corpus that did. We study this share for Gumbel-max and green-list (KGW) watermarks when every document may have been edited by an unknown amount. Without restrictions on the edits, the upper end of the identified set is 1. Combining a known least-favourable next-token distribution with a sequential coupling, we derive a power envelope indexed by three budgets: a retention floor, an entropy budget, and a budget for in-place substitutions, which for Gumbel-max make edited positions look less watermarked than human text. The envelope gives an identified set that is sharp for the alternatives it dominates, finite-sample bounds on the realized share of a fixed corpus with either the exact null or a null calibrated on an independent human sample, and confidence sequences for streams. In Gumbel-max simulations where the envelope binds, the bounds cover, and the threshold that maximizes the gap between envelope and null is generally as tight as twelve thresholds. On text from OPT-1.3B and Qwen3-1.7B, 12,000 fresh human documents scored under three keys show no robust departure from the exact null, and all bounds covered in resampling trials from finite pools of documents with random edits, substitutions and paraphrases; at a share of 0.2 the exact-null bound is about [0.14,0.38]. These results are fragile in two ways we make precise. LLM-paraphrased KGW pools fall below the envelope, yet at 2000 documents the bounds still covered in these trials. A simulated null error of 0.01, comparable to the largest deviation we observed at our thresholds, is likewise harmless at 2000 documents but breaks exact-null coverage at 100,000; a null calibrated on an independent human sample keeps coverage there, but needs a large reference sample to stay narrow.

134. 【2611.14975】Aggregate Accuracy Hides Diagnosis-Specific Failure in Medical Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14975

作者：Shreya Ajay Kale,Angad Ripudaman Singh Bajwa

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：hide complete failure, Medical vision-language models, open medical VLMs, single diagnosis, hide complete

备注： Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Medical Reasoning with Vision Language Foundation Models (Med-Reasoner). 16 pages including NeurIPS checklist, 1 figure, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are compared mainly by accuracy averaged across findings, a summary that can hide complete failure on a single diagnosis. We audit five open medical VLMs (MedGemma-4B, Lingshu-7B, HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MedVLM-R1) on 6,048 ChestX-ray14 questions balanced by sex, age, finding, and label. On the 432 pneumothorax-positive X-rays, HuatuoGPT-Vision and Qwen2.5-VL detect zero cases while MedGemma and Lingshu detect 38% and 67% of the same images. The two failing models answer "no" to all 864 pneumothorax cases (balanced accuracy 0.500, chance), yet produce fluent, anatomically specific explanations that name the signs of a collapsed lung and then deny them, with no hedge or abstention. This failure is not an artifact of prompting, image resolution, label ambiguity, or model scale. It persists across four prompt formats, at native resolution, and on single-label cases. A within-model control shows the same forced prompt gives HuatuoGPT 79% recall on nodule but only 6% on pneumothorax. It survives a tenfold scale increase (Qwen2.5-VL-72B detects 4%) and replicates on SIIM-ACR, an independent dataset with radiologist-drawn masks, where the failing models detect 0 of 432 confirmed pneumothoraces, including 0 of the 144 largest lesions. Reading the models' logits and internal states shows this is not blindness. The two failing models are highly confident yet no better than chance and a linear probe recovers pneumothorax from their hidden representations even where recall is zero, so the finding is represented internally but suppressed at the output. Aggregate accuracy therefore cannot establish that a medical VLM can see a given finding, because a model that misses one can still describe it convincingly. Reporting per-finding recall and specificity on balanced cases is needed to tell the difference.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Survey estimates for population segments, such as media use by sex and age, rest on few respondents per cell, and a growing literature offers synthetic respondents generated by large language models to fill the gap. That literature has not tested them against the small-area estimators a methodologist would otherwise use. We ran ten estimators, preregistered, on identical household-clustered splits of a Korean media panel across five calibration budgets and three holdout regimes. A multilevel partial-pooling model of the MRP family led every calibration-only estimator at every budget, matching at one hundred respondents what direct estimation reaches with roughly four times as many. On unobserved segments, information fixed as a per-segment prior was inert for synthetic panel and previous wave alike; attached as a bias correction it transferred, at the price of estimation noise. No registered synthetic advantage over MRP survived the paired decision rule. Entered instead as a covariate inside the multilevel model, an exploratory hybrid, the same panel improved on MRP on unobserved segments: the information survives where the registered constructions did not. Interval coverage stayed below nominal throughout and worsened with budget for partial pooling, contrary to prediction.

1. 【2611.17226】Sheaf-Inspired Structured Scoring Extensions of LightGCN for Collaborative Filtering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17226

作者：Ksenia Belkovskaya,Timur Lidzhiev,Maria Stebliy,Alexander Tarakanov

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computational Engineering, Finance, and Science (cs.CE); Computational Geometry (cs.CG); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Graph-based collaborative filtering, collaborative filtering learns, filtering learns user, Graph-based collaborative, item interaction graph

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Graph-based collaborative filtering learns user and item representations by propagating embeddings over a user--item interaction graph. We investigate a modular, sheaf-inspired scoring extension placed on top of a three-layer LightGCN backbone. The motivation comes from cellular sheaves, where local vector spaces are related by linear restriction maps; here, this idea motivates structured, pair-dependent transformations of user and item embeddings rather than a change to graph propagation. We compare the identity inner-product scorer, diagonal, rank-one low-rank, and full matrix residual scorers, and a parameter-matched residual MLP baseline (\texttt{mlp\_full}) on Amazon-Book, Gowalla, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp2018. We report ranking quality and catalog coverage at cutoffs 10 and 20. The selected structured variants show modest, dataset-dependent changes in ranking quality and sometimes substantial changes in coverage. Our method is inspired by sheaf geometry but does not construct a cellular sheaf or implement sheaf-Laplacian diffusion; the experiments therefore do not establish a causal benefit from sheaf geometry itself.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Automatic setlist identification aims to identify the songs performed in live concert recordings by matching noisy and variable-quality audio against a database of reference tracks. This task presents challenges beyond conventional music version identification, including substantial arrangement differences, background noise, and the need to process continuous recordings efficiently at large scale. Overall, the results demonstrate that segment-level audio embeddings provide a strong basis for scalable setlist identification and that compact representations can achieve strong accuracy under realistic live-concert conditions at much lower storage and serving cost.

3. 【2611.17030】Similar but Not Redundant: Embedding-Gated Refinement for High-Granularity Topic Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17030

作者：Daniel Denley,Robert L. Nicol,Mike J. Chantler

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：coherent themes fragment, modelling extracts hundreds, failure mode, topic modelling extracts, extracts hundreds

备注： 24 pages, 6 figures, 13 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:High-granularity topic modelling extracts hundreds of niche topics from a corpus, yet this regime is rarely studied and is more commonly framed as a failure mode: "over-clustering," in which coherent themes fragment into redundant subtopics. We argue that this framing conflates genuine redundancy with topics that are semantically similar but meaningfully distinct, and that the central challenge in high-granularity modelling is separating the two. Recent post-hoc refinement methods address topic redundancy through either embedding-based similarity thresholding or LLM-guided merging. We show that neither suffices in high-granularity settings and propose embedding-gated iterative merge (EGIM), a hybrid approach that iteratively generates context-rich topic representations, uses embedding similarity to propose candidate merges across the topic space, then applies LLM judgement to accept only genuinely redundant pairs while preserving niche distinctions. Across two patent corpora and one legal corpus, LLM judgement rejects 83% of embedding-identified candidates as non-redundant. EGIM improves discriminability on merged topics by 25-55% over a prior iterative LLM baseline while better preserving the original topic distribution.

4. 【2611.17012】Anonymous in Name Only: A Critical Review and a Reidentification Exercise on the European Commission's Anonymization Measures for Google Search Data under Article 6(11) of the DMA

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17012

作者：David Sánchez,Benet Manzanares,Josep Domingo-Ferrer

类目：Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Digital Markets Act, Markets Act, competing search engines, share Google Search, Digital Markets

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In July 2026, the European Commission (EC) set out the anonymization measures Alphabet must implement to share Google Search data with competing search engines under Article 6(11) of the Digital Markets Act. As the first concrete illustration of how the EC operationalizes anonymization under the EU digital rulebook, this methodology could become a regulatory benchmark. In this paper, we show why it should not. We critically review the anonymization measures proposed by the EC, from the characterization of residual risk to the suppression thresholds and the reliance on contractual safeguards. For each of those issues, we highlight significant conceptual flaws, and, where applicable, we illustrate these flaws with empirical observations from real Google Search data provided by Alphabet and protected under the EC's measures. Importantly, we present examples of low-effort reidentification, record linkage, and confidential attribute inference achieved through straightforward and automatable attack patterns. Our findings show that the approach of the EC relies on unjustified heuristics rather than formal privacy guarantees, underestimates the risks inherent in free-text search queries, and sacrifices data utility without achieving meaningful protection. We also demonstrate that the proposed measures do not meet any of the three anonymization criteria established by the European Data Protection Board (EDPB): no record isolation, no linkage, and no inference. Practitioners and regulators are therefore cautioned against treating these measures as a sound anonymization practice.

Additionally, we provide a rigorous benchmark that evaluates retrieval accuracy, diversity, and multi-task performance in one rubric system, where an effective advancement occurs if and only if the new method improves all metrics. 7. 【2611.16488】Active Learning of Approximate LLM Ranking for Energy-Efficient Retrieval of Long Queries

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16488

作者：Ohad Porat,Elad Yom-Tov

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Large Language Models, Large Language, offer complementary strengths, Language Models, Search engines

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Search engines and Large Language Models (LLMs) offer complementary strengths for document retrieval: lexical retrievers which rely on keyword matching are fast and energy-efficient, whereas LLMs capture semantic meaning and excel at interpreting long, context-rich queries. However, this comes at a cost that is computationally and energetically prohibitive. We propose the LLM Active Approximation Model (LAAM), a hybrid retrieval framework that introduces a lightweight learned predictor which serves as an intermediary between semantic retrieval and LLM retrieval. LAAM is refined through an active learning loop where an LLM labels a small number of documents. By concentrating LLM evaluations on the most informative documents, the framework substantially reduces inference cost while preserving retrieval effectiveness. We evaluate the proposed framework on TREC Robust 2004 using Lucene with BM25 retrieval and DeepSeek v3 as the LLM, under three levels of query verbosity. For verbose queries, LAAM achieves 87% of average P@10 achieved by DeepSeek's reranking (0.459 vs. 0.526), while requiring only 3.3% of the per-query energy consumption. LAAM is statistically significantly better than BM25 and is indistinguishable from DeepSeek on average precision, though not on MAP (Friedman test, P0.05 with post-hoc testing). Overall, our results demonstrate that selectively approximating LLM relevance judgments through active learning enables retrieval accuracy to be controlled directly through the evaluation (and consequently energy) budget. Rather than committing to either inexpensive (and less accurate) lexical retrieval or exhaustive (and expensive) LLM reranking, our framework exposes a continuous spectrum of operating points along the energy--accuracy curve, allowing practitioners to select how to trade retrieval quality with computational budget.

备注： 13 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Discriminative embeddings encode original multimodal inputs efficiently but struggle with fine-grained semantics. Generative embeddings use reasoning to capture query intent and relevance evidence, but incur higher computational costs. We propose AntEmbed, a dual-mode multimodal embedding model that supports efficient retrieval and reasoning-enhanced fine-grained matching in a unified embedding space. First, we construct retrieval-oriented reasoning content that captures query intent and confusion-aware semantic evidence. We treat original and reasoning-enhanced candidate inputs as semantically equivalent views, and use multi-positive contrastive learning with a single embedding token to align discriminative and generative embeddings. Second, cross-mode self-distillation transfers fine-grained semantic information from reasoning-enhanced to original representations through candidate similarity-distribution distillation and query representation alignment. This strengthens discriminative embeddings without requiring additional online reasoning. Both retrieval modes share a single candidate index. On MMEB-v2, AntEmbed-8B achieves an overall score of 81.5 with reasoning-enhanced embeddings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.1 points.

10. 【2611.16404】ReSID: Rethinking Long-Tail Retrieval in Generative Recommendation through Item Reachability

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16404

作者：Muyu Zou,Shunye Tang,Yi Xu,Haibo Xing,Yandong Wang,Lingyu Mu,Jinxin Hu

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：discrete Semantic IDs, Generative recommendation, Semantic IDs, discrete Semantic, Generative

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative recommendation (GR) represents items with discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and casts retrieval as sequence generation. Under skewed interaction distributions and tight serving budgets, GR struggles to retrieve long-tail items. In this work, we are the first to study long-tail reachability across the complete pipeline of SID construction, decoding, and matching. We find that under equal code lengths, parallel SID construction consistently outperforms sequential SIDs on tail items. Moreover, even when full generation fails, decoded sequences carry locally consistent information that retrieves relevant tail items via partial matching. Yet this recovery is bounded by upstream search, failing once beam search prunes all prefixes completable within a fixed mismatch tolerance. Motivated by these findings, we propose ReSID to jointly preserve and recover tail candidates. Built on long parallel SIDs, ReSID pairs popularity-debiased and user-semantic-aware beam search to keep promising paths alive with partial SID matching to recover items from surviving tokens. ReSID achieves state-of-the-art long-tail retrieval while maintaining competitive overall accuracy, with lower latency and higher throughput under narrow beams. ReSID is deployed in our production system, serving main user traffic with substantial business gains. We release our complete code and model checkpoints to support full reproducibility and real-world deployment at this https URL.

11. 【2611.16395】riGen: A Generative Paradigm for Trigger-Induced Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16395

作者：Muyu Zou,Haibo Xing,Shunye Tang,Liyan Tie,Lingyu Mu,Yandong Wang,Jinxin Hu

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：predicting target items, autoregressively generating semantic, generating semantic IDs, recommendation typically operates, predicting target

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative recommendation typically operates under the User-Induced Recommendation (UIR) setting, predicting target items by autoregressively generating semantic IDs as a passive continuation of long-term user history. However, this paradigm overlooks Trigger-Induced Recommendation (TIR), an essential industrial scenario where a user click triggers an immediate follow-up feed. In TIR, recommendations must dynamically align with the instant trigger intent without compromising historical preference continuity. Traditional TIR methods rely on discriminative scoring and cannot guide token-level generation, while existing generative models lack mechanisms to reconcile this structural tension. To address this challenge, we propose TriGen, establishing a tailored generative paradigm for trigger-induced recommendation. TriGen introduces calibrated tail injection to re-encode local history, adaptively arbitrates between persistent tastes and immediate impulses via dynamic gating, and applies position-specific transformations to sustain level-aware trigger guidance across decoding steps. Extensive offline evaluations confirm consistent gains over competitive baselines. Furthermore, online A/B tests across six country markets demonstrate significant revenue and GMV improvements, leading to the full production deployment of TriGen.

12. 【2611.16366】CURATOR: Scalable Fine-grained Evidence Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation using Decision Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16366

作者：Shib Dasgupta,Bandish Parikh,Rashmi Gangadharaiah

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Retrieval-augmented generation, entire retrieved passages, Retrieval-augmented, LLM, spans

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides entire retrieved passages to the LLM, even though only a handful of spans within them contain the evidence pertaining to the question. While precise selection of these spans can improve context utilization, identifying them in isolation is challenging. A span may appear irrelevant by itself, and deciding whether to retain it may require examining other retrieved information. One can evaluate each span's candidacy with the full retrieved context, but this is computationally inefficient as the same text must be processed repeatedly. Pretrained decision model can be used to efficiently evaluate multiple spans simultaneously, allowing each decision to consider the surrounding spans as well. However, our experiments show that their ability to identify relevant evidence deteriorates as the number of simultaneous decisions increases. To address this, we introduce CURATOR, a fine-tuned decision model trained to make reliable evidence-selection decisions over large candidate sets. By training a decision model to select varying numbers of supporting spans from large candidate pools, CURATOR develops the capability to select evidence for RAG while retaining the computational efficiency of joint decision-making. We observe that CURATOR maintains a strong evidence discrimination capability as the number of candidates increases, generalizes across different tasks and document domains, and improves downstream RAG performance, particularly when the available context is limited.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Social-media event intelligence requires a system to determine whether a post belongs to a registered event space, identify the supported hierarchy path, and stop at the deepest level warranted by the evidence. We formulate this requirement as open-set hierarchical event linking over frozen Storyline--Episode--Atomic Event Cards. GA-HEL implements a three-stage, knowledge-driven expert-system funnel: open-set scope control limits candidate exposure before hierarchical reasoning, hierarchy-aware decoders select a supported path and depth, and deterministic legal-path validation checks each record before database-compatible use. A 200-post Pilot supports task and mechanism development, while a separately annotated 497-post Expansion set evaluates transfer across four held-out Storylines and a hard-negative pool. Method implementations, prompts, Event Cards, candidate order, and six systems' predictions were frozen before Expansion Gold annotation. The resulting evaluation combines end-to-end hierarchical matching, open-set rejection, structural validation, ablation, cross-Storyline analysis, and an independent expert reannotation of a fixed audited subset. GA-HEL remains competitive with strong global full-card reasoning while providing explicit scope control and hierarchy-valid structured records. Error analysis identifies unsupported descent from Episode-level evidence to Atomic Events as the dominant remaining challenge. The task definition, frozen resource, and auditable protocol provide a reproducible foundation for scope-aware event intelligence. Code, Event Cards, labels, frozen predictions, and evaluation scripts are available at this https URL.

14. 【2611.16159】From Tokens to Behaviors: Rethinking Generative Recommendation for User Trajectory Prediction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16159

作者：Jiaqi Wang,Hanchen Yang,Hengyu Zhang,Hongxu Ma,Wenqi Rao,Hongyang Wang,Zhongbo Sun,Renliang Qian,Weixin Chen,Wengen Li,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Hong Cheng

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：aims to capture, evolve over time, time by predicting, predicting the sequence, sequence of future

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:User behavior trajectory prediction (UBTP) aims to capture how a user's needs evolve over time by predicting the sequence of future interactions from that user's interaction history. Recent generative recommendation (GR) methods address this task through autoregressive generation of semantic IDs (SIDs). However, serial SID decoding couples token generation with behavior state transitions and requires multiple dependent steps per interaction. Discrete item feedback overlooks predictive uncertainty, and teacher-forced training leaves a training-inference gap in trajectory generation. To address these limitations, we propose TraGR, a behavior-aware generative recommendation framework that aligns generation, feedback, and learning with successive user behaviors. Its behavior-level architecture predicts SID tokens in parallel within each interaction and models behavioral transitions through recurrent state updates, with joint SID and item-level supervision. Probability feedback combines the selected behavior with a probability-weighted summary of alternatives to guide subsequent predictions. Relation-aware trajectory learning incorporates collaborative transition signals and later credit assignment into training on model-generated trajectories. Experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that TraGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different prediction horizons. Code: this https URL.

15. 【2611.16149】HyperPage: Learned Page Layouts for Disk-Resident Graph Vector Indexes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16149

作者：Xinyu Zhang,Jingze Li,Baihua Zheng,Weiwei Sun

类目：Databases (cs.DB); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Disk-resident graph indexes, Disk-resident graph, SSD pages, layout, DiskANN store

备注： 14 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Code available at: [this https URL](https://github.com/zhangshea/HyperPage)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Disk-resident graph indexes such as DiskANN store each vector with its graph edges as one record on SSD pages. A search needs the page of every record it expands, so its page reads depend on the page layout, that is, which records share a page. Search agents trained with reinforcement learning query one such index run after run, and every graph traversal can be logged with the records it expands. We therefore use the logs of a finished run to lay out the index for a declared memory budget, so later runs read fewer pages. In contrast, existing layouts such as Starling and MARGO are computed from the graph and vectors and use no logs. Learning a layout from logs is challenging, because the logs must stay valid under every layout, the saving depends on what the budget caches, and the best layout is NP-hard to find. Our key observation is that DiskANN-style search routes on in-memory codes and the records it expands, never on their page-mates. As a result, the logged traversals price every layout by replay, and cached records add no page reads. Accordingly, we propose HyperPage, which first builds layouts for the declared budget on the logged traversals of uncached records. It then selects one layout, together with the budget split between hot records and a page cache, by replaying validation steps. In addition, a Lagrangian lower bound shows how far a layout may be from the optimum. We learn from one Search-R1 agent run and test on a later run with a different reward, over 1M and 21M passages. Across seven cache budgets, HyperPage reads 5.2% to 21.2% fewer pages than MARGO, and no compared layout reads fewer. The advantage is largest when the cache is small. For example, at 1% of the 1M index, MARGO needs nearly 3 times the budget to read as few pages. On the 21M index, HyperPage also cuts search time per step by up to 10.4%. The saving holds for a second agent model and a second corpus.

16. 【2611.16148】Calibrated Early Termination with Bounded Optimality Gaps for Constrained Vector Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16148

作者：Xinyu Zhang,Jingze Li,Baihua Zheng,Weiwei Sun

类目：Databases (cs.DB); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Vector search, retrieval-augmented generation, category may contribute, retrieval layer, layer of recommendation

备注： 14 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Code available at [this https URL](https://github.com/zhangshea/CaST)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vector search over learned embeddings is the retrieval layer of recommendation and retrieval-augmented generation, and many applications cap how many answer items one category may contribute. Graph indexes answer a query by best-first traversal, and where it stops determines its cost and whether its answer is exact. We study early termination for such constrained $k$-nearest-neighbor ($k$-NN) queries, with plain $k$-NN as a special case, and seek the cheapest stopping rule whose answer is exact with probability at least $1-\varepsilon$. Learned early-termination methods instead target average recall, which does not guarantee exact answers. Multiple testing can calibrate any parameterized stopping rule to our target, but it cannot tell how much a better rule with the same risk and observations would save. Answering this needs a lower bound on the optimum of a rule family too large to enumerate, valid for the query population. Our key idea is to declare the observations a rule may use and to consider every rule monotone in them. Risk-constrained stopping then becomes a linear program over an order polytope, whose Lagrangian minimizers form a nested chain of staircase rules computed exactly by dynamic programming. Fixed-sequence binomial tests along this chain calibrate our rule, CaST, whose failure probability is at most $\varepsilon$ with confidence $1-\delta$. Lagrangian weak duality bounds the optimum of the declared class from below at all risk levels at once, without a union bound over the rules. On two datasets of one million items each, CaST replayed on logged searches costs at most 1.132 times the class optimum for risk levels from 0.03 to 0.10, at 95% joint confidence per setting. Larger savings thus need a rule outside the class. At a common risk target on held-out queries, CaST is faster than every other calibrated rule we time, which take 4.8% to 45.5% more mean time.

17. 【2611.16060】REWEAVE: Online Learning for Workload-Adaptive HNSW Indexes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16060

作者：Minghao Li,Nick Koudas,Raghav Mittal

类目：Databases (cs.DB); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Navigable Small World, Hierarchical Navigable Small, Small World, Hierarchical Navigable, Navigable Small

备注： 20 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables. Full version with appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vector databases rely on Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) indexes for similarity search, but a graph built from data alone can be poorly matched to a skewed or changing query workload. We present REWEAVE (Reward-Guided Rewiring of Vector Graphs), an online index-maintenance method that learns edge utility from query feedback and periodically rewires the bottom layer within the original per-node degree cap. The method supports sparse exact-neighbor feedback combined with self-labels, and standard HNSW serving without learning overhead once adaptation is disabled. Under explicit assumptions we prove value-estimation and graph-stabilization guarantees. Under stationary skew, REWEAVE improves held-out recall by up to 23.3 percentage points over HNSW. In comparisons with query-aware baselines on up to 100M vectors, it achieves up to 3.3x NGFix's query throughput at recall@100 of 0.80. On Amazon-ESCI with query drift, it increases stream recall@100 from 0.745 to 0.979 with 34% fewer search distance computations than frozen HNSW. These results illustrate the potential to improve retrieval quality and reduce search effort within an existing graph-degree budget, while incurring additional work during adaptation.

18. 【2611.16053】Wayfinder: Adaptive Resource Routing from Agent Citations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16053

作者：Miguel Romero,George Karypis

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：issue trackers, act effectively, email and knowledge, knowledge bases, project meeting

备注： 20 pages, 3 figures, 10 tables. Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:To act effectively, agents must gather contextual information across resources such as chats, issue trackers, email and knowledge bases, to answer questions like "what was decided in the last project meeting?" or "where is the FAQ for our internal tooling?" Where this information lives in a user's environment is unknown to the agent, so it re-explores the same resources across consecutive interactions. Common routing approaches represent each resource as a tool and rely on high-level descriptions to generalize to unseen environments. Such descriptions often fail to separate similar or overlapping resources and cannot improve from the agent's subsequent interactions. We introduce Wayfinder, an adaptive router that uses the documents cited by the downstream agent as feedback about where relevant information resides. These citations update Wayfinder's internal resource representations, which subsequent interactions use to prioritize the resources likely to contain relevant information. We also introduce resource augmentation during offline reinforcement learning, which anonymizes and permutes resource identities to discourage training-time overfitting of which resource holds which topic; our ablations suggest it helps most when few training environments are available. Because no openly available professional environments pair realistic queries with resource-access patterns, we construct and release three controlled testbeds derived from existing datasets: NFCorpus, FEVER and Tech Startup. Compared with supervised, tool-retrieval and contextual bandit baselines under a shared closed-loop protocol, Wayfinder gives the best routing when resources contain semantically overlapping content that static descriptions cannot distinguish, while description-based tool retrieval suffices when resources are drawn from distinct corpora.

Abstract:Cultural heritage sites are increasingly monitored by sensor networks, but querying their data requires database expertise. 22. 【2611.15597】When Local Definitions Are Missing: Stable Errors in Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15597

作者：Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：technical manuals, online communities, words special meanings, definition, special meanings

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Contracts, technical manuals, and online communities often give familiar words special meanings and define them in one place. A language model that reads a passage without that definition may use the usual meaning and give an answer that sounds right but is wrong. We study this with multiple-choice questions whose correct answers depend on a local definition. The questions come from a synthetic vocabulary, commercial contracts, Reddit community glossaries, and the documentation of a decentralized exchange. We ask three language models each question with and without the definition. Without using the answer key, we then test whether their repeated answers and stated confidence reveal their own errors. Separately, we give a small open model the answer key and test whether it can predict which questions larger models get wrong. We report three findings. (1) Familiar words that a source redefines cause the most errors. Models often repeat the same wrong answer, and neither repeated answers nor stated confidence reliably flag it. (2) The source definition fixes most errors on synthetic questions, but a definition drawn from the model's own prior knowledge does not. In real contracts, the source definition fixes only about half of the errors. (3) Given an answer key, a small model can rank questions by how likely larger models are to get them wrong. These results call for tests that check answers against the full source, including its local definitions.

23. 【2611.15445】ReAlign: Reward-Guided Preference Alignment for Generative Re-Ranking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15445

作者：YaChen Yan,Liubo Li

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Re-ranking refines, upstream ranked list, maximize listwise user, listwise utility signal, listwise user utility

备注： 9 pages, 3 figures, ACM conference format

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Re-ranking refines the upstream ranked list to maximize listwise user utility. Existing two-stage generator-evaluator methods decouple list generation from list evaluation, leaving the generator without direct access to the listwise utility signal, while scoring-based one-stage methods score items under a fixed upstream permutation context without explicitly searching the permutation space. We propose \textbf{ReAlign}, a reward-guided preference alignment framework that couples a listwise reward model with a candidate-aware autoregressive generator. The reward model decomposes the listwise reward into position-wise contributions, modeling user exposure through a survival-based exposure module and item value through per-objective heads for heterogeneous engagement signals. The generator is trained in two stages: supervised pre-training on logged exposure lists to establish a relevance prior, followed by reward-guided post-training that aligns the generator with the reward model through complementary distribution and ranking alignment over within-pool standardized rewards. Extensive offline experiments show that ReAlign consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines.

24. 【2611.15434】SurvEval: Listwise Re-Ranking Evaluator via Survival-Based Exposure and Multi-Objective Value Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15434

作者：YaChen Yan,Liubo Li

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：existing evaluators oversimplify, reflect true listwise, collapse multi-objective signals, evaluators oversimplify user, oversimplify user browsing

备注： 5 Pages, 3 figures, ACM conference format

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Re-ranking is a crucial stage in recommender systems, yet existing evaluators oversimplify user browsing assumptions and collapse multi-objective signals into a single scalar, limiting their ability to reflect true listwise utility. We propose \textbf{SurvEval}, a listwise evaluator that jointly models user exposure and item value for re-ranking evaluation. We formalize the sequential browsing process as a discrete-time survival model, deriving monotonically decreasing reaching probabilities that capture the cascading structure of user browsing. We further introduce a multi-objective value estimation module with dedicated per-objective prediction heads and uncertainty-based loss weighting to adaptively balance objectives of varying scales and noise levels. Both components are jointly optimized via a unified transformer backbone, and the listwise utility is obtained by summing the position-wise products of reaching probabilities and conditional item value estimates. Extensive experiments on KuaiRand and Avito demonstrate that SurvEval achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

26. 【2611.15417】When Direction Matters: Diagnosing Relational Instruction Following in Dense Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15417

作者：Zhanyu Wu,Richong Zhang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Dense retrievers increasingly, natural-language relevance criteria, retrievers increasingly claim, Dense retrievers, increasingly claim

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dense retrievers increasingly claim the ability to condition retrieval on natural-language relevance criteria beyond a short query. We identify a specific weakness in this capability: the evaluated models perform well when lexical or attribute cues distinguish the target, but become much less reliable when relevance depends on a directional relation over the same entities. We introduce SwitchIR, a controlled diagnostic that contrasts Constraint Shift and Explicit Filtering with Relational Flip, where the query and document pair remain fixed while the requested relation direction reverses. Across representative embedding and late-interaction retrievers, the primary joint metric is high on the two cue-available controls and consistently low on Relational Flip; the same ordering persists in an LLM-assisted, manually reviewed realistic variant. An exact margin decomposition shows that both directions are correct only when the direction-sensitive query--document interaction exceeds the direction-invariant pair preference. Empirically, direction-sensitive changes are common, but their aligned interaction is usually too weak to overcome that preference. Diagnostic fine-tuning shows that models can learn relational behavior that generalizes across relation families, with stronger generalization in the larger model.

29. 【2611.14938】When Does a Recommendation Spark Curiosity? Theory, Measurement, and Field Evidence

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14938

作者：Pan Li,Alexander Tuzhilin

类目：General Economics (econ.GN); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：sustains engagement, relevance-only recommendations, Curiosity, familiar content, bubbles of repetitive

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Curiosity, the drive to close the gap between what one knows and what one wants to know, pulls consumers beyond their familiar content, sustains engagement, and breaks them out of the filter bubbles of repetitive, relevance-only recommendations. Despite its importance, curiosity is hardly incorporated into modern recommender systems for lack of a formal measure. The information-gap theory of curiosity provides no formal quantitative model that a recommender system can operate on, and existing proxy metrics do not accurately measure the curiosity that a recommended item evokes. To fill this research gap, we formalize the information-gap theory into five axioms of curiosity and construct the canonical formulation of a computational measure of the curiosity that a recommended item evokes in a consumer. We validate our curiosity measure through a survey-based study, in which it matches consumers' self-reported curiosity and outperforms alternative proxy metrics. We subsequently build a curiosity-aware recommender system that incorporates this measure into the utility function design, and we show through offline experiments on three real-world datasets that it significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, we conduct a large-scale online controlled experiment at a major video-streaming platform in Asia, where we show that our method significantly increases click-through rate, video views, and time spent over the latest production system, leading to tangible economic impact for the company. Our method has since been deployed on the platform and remains in operation. Our findings establish consumer curiosity as a measurable and actionable objective for recommender systems, with important managerial implications for digital platforms to recommend what consumers wonder about.

We reanalyzed datasets spanning experimental EBV infection, CNS-homing B cells in humanized mice, early-MS blood, and paired human CSF and blood. A frozen EBV-induced B-cell factor (F12) was transferred without outcome-driven modification and evaluated using anatomical, sequencing-depth, metabolic, coherence-matched, lineage, and exposure controls. F12 did not predict MS activity (AUC=0.487; p=0.489) and was lower in CSF in nine evaluable participants (mean difference -0.0560; coherence-matched p=0.015). It also remained lower in brain than spleen in all five EBV-exposed mice after matched-depth rarefaction (p=0.0313) and coherence-matched calibration (p=0.0225). The splenic TBX21+CXCR3+ transcriptional gate did not reliably represent the state of anatomically selected brain B cells. An enriched 287-gene cross-system bridge lacked B-lineage and EBV-exposure specificity. In human CSF, an early EBV-associated program was depleted (-0.0492; p=0.0197), whereas latency-III and lymphoblastoid cell line-like programs increased (+0.0666; p=0.0092 and +0.0148; p=0.0096, respectively). Opposite early and late program directions persisted after removing shared genes. No external program met the prespecified prospective criterion. These findings support stage- and compartment-dependent remodeling of EBV-associated B-cell transcription rather than persistence of a stable RNA signature. They distinguish cellular trafficking from transcriptional-state preservation and show that cross-system molecular resemblance alone cannot establish ancestry. EBV-directed biomarker studies should integrate host transcription with virologic, clonotypic, antigen-specific, epigenetic, and longitudinal evidence.

1. 【2611.17336】Back to Pixels: Lifting Latent Video Diffusion into Pixel Space

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17336

作者：Yanke Song,Shuo Chen,Chris Rockwell,Guande He,Nan Liu,Desai Xie,Tianpei Gu,Yong-Hyun Park,Aryan V S,Dennis Yuren Menn,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Xun Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Frontier video generation, generation models work, Frontier video, Frontier, VAE latent space

备注： Project Page: [this https URL](https://chenshuo20.github.io/PixVid/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Frontier video generation models work in a VAE latent space that compresses video in both space and time. This compression made training tractable, but now limits high-resolution, controllable, and interactive generation -- doubling resolution quadruples token count, conditions apply to groups of frames rather than individual ones, and each response must wait for the rest of its multi-frame block. We present PixVid, a simple yet effective method that lifts a pretrained latent video model to directly generate every frame in pixel space. PixVid keeps the diffusion transformer and replaces the VAE decoder with a convolutional head that renders its outputs into pixels. We find that predicting clean video rather than velocity, together with a larger noise shift, is key to quality. Finetuned from Wan2.1-14B, PixVid is competitive with the latent baseline within $\sim 1.3$M training clips. Working framewise in pixels, it generates at $2\times$ higher resolution with the same per-frame token count, keeps depth-conditioned video editing aligned under fast motion, and responds to each action at the next frame. Project page: this https URL

2. 【2611.17334】Unbundling Multimodal Vision Encoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17334

作者：Ellis Brown,Chris Hoang,Shengyi Qian,Shang-Wen Li,Mike Lewis,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Luke Zettlemoyer,Lucas Beyer,Mengye Ren,Jakob Verbeek,Saining Xie,Hu Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：encode visual input, visual input remains, input remains, vision, encoder

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://unbundling.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:How multimodal language models should encode visual input remains up for debate. Most models use a pretrained vision encoder, while some recent recipes train the encoder from scratch with the language model or remove it entirely. Vision encoders bundle together three design choices: a visual prior from upstream pretraining, dedicated vision capacity, and pre-fusion placement of that capacity (i.e., before the multimodal backbone). Because published models also differ in data, architecture, and compute, comparing them cannot isolate the effect of each choice. We disentangle these choices via a controlled framework where we pretrain all models from scratch on common data and compare them on visual question answering and language benchmarks at matched lifetime compute. We find that the visual prior is not necessary; an encoder trained jointly from scratch is on par with a pretrained one, without a separate training stage. What matters instead is dedicated vision capacity: an encoder-free model trails on visual understanding even when its backbone is enlarged by the encoder's parameter count. Yet that capacity need not precede multimodal fusion, as vision-only modules added in-fusion to early backbone layers perform comparably to a pre-fusion encoder and can lower time to first token. These results also hold across backbone sizes, when encoder pretraining is treated as free, and when starting from a pretrained language model. Overall, our findings favor dedicated vision capacity trained jointly with the backbone and placed early within it.

3. 【2611.17331】B: Test-time Baking for Efficient Rendering of Decoder-only View Synthesis Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17331

作者：Chin-Yang Lin,Ryo Hachiuma,Jaesung Choe,Min-Hung Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu,Cheng Sun

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Decoder-only Large View, View Synthesis Models, Large View Synthesis, Decoder-only Large, Synthesis Models

备注： NeurIPS 2026 (Spotlight). Project page: [this https URL](https://test-time-baking.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Decoder-only Large View Synthesis Models (LVSMs) with KV-cache have recently achieved state-of-the-art quality by regressing novel views from neural networks without reconstructing 3D geometry, while Test-Time Training (TTT) layers save computation but sacrifice quality. We propose Test-Time Baking (TTB), a strategy that learns to bake global scene information from cross-view attention into a lightweight MLP, enabling fast and high-fidelity novel-view decoding. The first key is to repurpose the fast-weight MLP of TTT to learn the cross-view attention mapping explicitly from its input-output pairs, rather than replacing attention entirely, achieving the same rendering efficiency as TTT while delivering substantially higher quality. The second key addresses a modality misalignment between context prefilling and novel-view rendering: we disentangle context tokens into camera-only context rendering tokens to serve as TTB input, and context ground-truth tokens with image content to serve as the TTB mapping target, ensuring the baked MLPs are relieved of the modality generalization challenge between context and novel-view processing. Our method achieves up to **23x** rendering speedup with quality on par with the state-of-the-art across several benchmarks. Project page: this https URL

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision language navigation (VLN) is very challenging for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) since small errors in 3D motion prediction can lead to navigation errors and collisions. Unable to fathom execution outcomes in 3D environments, existing methods often struggle to predict accurate navigation waypoints from language instructions. To solve this, we introduce the Prototypical Visual Imagination framework, shortened as ProtoVis-Nav, which forces the model to imagine an outcome-associated local visual representation for the intended next UAV movement. 8. 【2611.17302】FLEX-LBP: Learning Local Binary Patterns with Exact Binary Inference for Texture Classification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17302

作者：Mohamed Alimoussa,Amal El Kaid

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Local Binary Patterns (LBP) remain among the most widely used texture descriptors, from industrial inspection to medical imaging. Yet their radius, sampling angles, color transform and thresholds are fixed by hand for each dataset. We present FLEX-LBP, which learns these parameters by backpropagation while every forward pass computes exact binary codes and hard histogram counts. A straight-through estimator acts only in the backward pass, so the deployed descriptor is an interpretable hard-binary LBP with 90 learnable parameters and unchanged inference cost. In a controlled ablation on six color texture benchmarks, the component worth learning is dataset-dependent. It improves the nearest-neighbor accuracy of the identical fixed descriptor by 3.55 points on average over five seeds, significantly on five of six benchmarks and never significantly worse. FLEX-LBP is more accurate than five classical LBP-family descriptors, PCANet and a neural LBP on five of six benchmarks. Its mean nearest-neighbor accuracy exceeds that of frozen ImageNet ResNet-50 features (23.5 million parameters). On the Kather colorectal cancer histology benchmark, the fully learned descriptor reaches 93.8%, significantly above the best classical descriptor (Completed LBP, 92.8%). The learned radii adapt in directions that recur across seeds. Noise drowns single-pixel comparisons and illuminant change flips inter-channel ones; median sampling or gray-world normalization restores 96-97%. The code is available as a drop-in PyTorch layer at this https URL.

9. 【2611.17298】Consistent Video-to-Video Translation via Explicit Correspondences

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17298
