点击查看摘要

Abstract:Interactive video-to-video applications require real-time generation while maintaining long-range temporal consistency. However, recent methods achieve speed by restricting attention to a fixed-length causal window. This limits long-range consistency whenever relevant history falls outside the context window. To address this limitation, we introduce vid2vid-long, a correspondence-based approach grounded in a simple observation. Video-to-video translation provides an explicit cross-frame reference: the regions that match in the input video should match in the output video too. Concretely, we compute patch-level correspondences in the input, guiding retrieval of previously generated outputs in the corresponding regions, and condition the model on these patches through correspondence cross-attention. Our experiments demonstrate that vid2vid-long consistently improves long-range temporal consistency across recent video models while preserving generation quality and real-time performance.

10. 【2611.17240】Objects as Addresses: Instance Binding in Vision Transformer Attention

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17240

作者：Khalil Sabri,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Nicolas Saunier,Wassim Bouachir

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Humans naturally parse, naturally parse visual, parse visual scenes, capability cognitive science, cognitive science calls

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Humans naturally parse visual scenes into coherent individual objects rather than scattered features, a capability cognitive science calls object binding. While recent work shows that pretrained Vision Transformers (ViTs) intrinsically acquire this ability, how native self-attention computations mechanistically keep object instances separate remains unresolved. By studying this across eight pretrained ViTs, we found that instance separation is expressed more strongly in attention routing than in token content: selected query-key relations distinguish individual objects more reliably than value similarity or residual representations. Inside heads, patches belonging to one individual tend to read from a common image region, which we term their attention address. Visually distinct parts of one person share an address, whereas identical duplicated instances receive separate, movable addresses. These shared destinations arise in part because query interactions with the spatial structure of keys direct attention toward consistent regions for one object and elsewhere for another. In DINOv2 and most other backbones we tested, editing this query component at the selected head redirects its attention address toward another individual. Grouping patches by this query component separates same-category instances, reaching .615 FG-ARI versus .289 for the same clustering of final-layer features, which tend to merge them.

11. 【2611.17199】MALNISI-Infused Graph Construction and Weighted Contrastive Loss based GAT for Unsupervised Representation Learning in HSI

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17199

作者：Soumi Sarkar,Subhadip Boral,Sarbani Palit,Ashish Ghosh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：land utilization due, possess discriminating spectral, discriminating spectral characteristics, possess discriminating, characteristics with respect

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Hyperspectral images (HSI) possess discriminating spectral characteristics with respect to land utilization due to high-dimensional information. Recently, unsupervised representation learning using graph neural networks has unveiled these utilization-specific complex spectral relations. This article has three main contributions. First, it proposes an index, the Mean Absolute Local Neighborhood Inverse Spectral Index (MALNISI), which measures the similarity between two pixels based on their neighborhood information. The index is used to construct a weighted adjacency matrix, giving the HSI a graph structure. Second, it proposes an upgraded Graph Attention Network that learns compact latent representations of hyperspectral data from the constructed graph. In this network, an enhanced multi-head attention mechanism incorporates the MALNISI weights as an auxiliary feature to emphasize learned attention between neighboring pixels. To alleviate over-smoothing and preserve fine-grained spectral details, the architecture integrates residual connections, skip projections, and feature normalization, which together enhance representation capability, stabilize learning, and accelerate convergence. Third, it proposes a weighted contrastive loss function that incorporates neighborhood-aware MALNISI between connected pixels, encouraging strongly connected pixels in the high-dimensional space to remain close in the latent space, and weakly connected pixels to stay apart. The resulting discriminative low-dimensional embeddings are then clustered using spectral clustering. Experimental analysis on six benchmark HSI datasets demonstrates the efficiency and necessity of the three aforementioned contributions, and the comparative study highlights superior performance over fourteen state-of-the-art approaches.

12. 【2611.17194】DMD: Endpoint Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17194

作者：Tong Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Yucheng Han,Ruoyu Wang,Mingkun Lei,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Distribution matching distillation, student final output, student final outputs, distilling diffusion models, score model tracks

备注： Code Link: see [this https URL](https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/eDMD)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Distribution matching distillation (DMD) has emerged as a leading paradigm for distilling diffusion models into few-step students, where an auxiliary fake score model tracks the student's distribution and the discrepancy between the teacher and fake predictions drives the student toward the teacher. In multi-step distillation, although only the student's final output is delivered, existing methods train the fake model on intermediate states or across all denoising steps. Consequently, the student's update signal becomes entangled with intermediate distributions, which can distort the matching direction and remain nonzero even after the student's final outputs already match the teacher distribution. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Endpoint Distribution Matching Distillation (eDMD), which trains a single shared fake model exclusively on the student's final outputs and uses it to correct every denoising step. Our theoretical analysis proves that this endpoint reference guarantees that the distribution matching term vanishes if and only if the student's final output distribution matches the teacher's, regardless of intermediate distributions. Beyond the fake reference, we further strengthen teacher supervision through a precise teacher (PT) that replaces single-step guided predictions with short multi-step trajectories on the final noise interval, paired with a calibrated query density for intermediate steps. Experiments on SD3-Medium, FLUX.2-klein (4B and 9B), and Qwen-Image-2.1 demonstrate that eDMD consistently outperforms existing distribution matching methods for 4-step generation.

14. 【2611.17185】Neural Render Proxies for Interactive and Differentiable Lighting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17185

作者：Sergio Sancho,Alexander Rath,Marco Manzi,Pascal Chang,Amit H. Bermano,Derek Nowrouzezahrai,Markus Gross,Marios Papas

类目：Graphics (cs.GR); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：animation production pipeline, challenges artists tackle, production pipeline, animation production, challenges artists

备注： Accepted at Computer Graphics Forum (Proc. EGSR 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Within the CG animation production pipeline, the challenges artists tackle in Lighting can be immense. Even minor adjustments require re-rendering massive scenes with slow offline renderers; global illumination has to be sampled and complex shaders have to be evaluated, leading to iteration times of minutes to hours per frame. To accelerate this process, we introduce a novel neural render proxy (NRP) that enables differentiable relighting of static scenes with fixed camera and materials at interactive rates. Our main insight is the decoupling of traditional rendering into path sampling and emission computation. From a single, light-agnostic render pass, we collect light transport data in the form of path samples. This enables rapidly sampling lighting conditions on the fly, and training a scene-specific lightweight neural network that learns how light is transported from any scene location to any image pixel. This approach is compatible with non-differentiable production renderers, induces minimal memory requirements during inference, and scales only with resolution and the number of light sources and parameters, but independently of scene and appearance complexity. Our evaluation demonstrates interactive frame rates for relighting (~30-60 Hz) while closely approximating the visual fidelity of ground-truth path tracing. In addition, the differentiable NRP enables fast, gradient-based inverse workflows, allowing artists to efficiently solve for lighting parameters from intuitive image-space edits or generative targets.

16. 【2611.17172】SpotDiff: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Zero-Shot Change Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17172

作者：Anit Upadhyaya,Ata Akbari Asanjan,Chaitanya Jalluri,Subash Yeggina,Jeremy Kravitz

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Remote sensing change detection usually relies on large collections of temporally aligned, pixel-level annotations, which limits its scalability across regions, imaging platforms and application domains. Recent zero-shot approaches reduce the need for supervision, but they often localize fine-grained changes poorly because their features are coarse or inconsistent across time. We present SpotDiff, a training-free, plug-and-play framework for zero-shot change detection built by composing complementary vision foundation models. SpotDiff uses DINOv3 for semantically robust features, AnyUp to reconstruct them at pixel resolution, and SAM 3 for text-guided region-of-interest proposals, which an inference-time multi-scale strategy (SWIMS) makes reliable on small objects. Change is then measured by comparing the reconstructed features of the two dates inside the proposed regions, with no learnable components, fine-tuning or change labels. On LEVIR-CD, SpotDiff reaches 82.46 F1, 2.06 points above the strongest prior training-free method, and on the multi-class SECOND benchmark it improves building change detection by 10.3 IoU and the six-class average by 2.2 IoU. Qualitative results on PlanetScope (3 m) and Sentinel-2 (10 m) imagery show that the same pipeline transfers across resolutions without modification. Our results indicate that composing modern foundation models is an effective route to scalable, label-free change detection.

17. 【2611.17163】UAV-Flow++: A Unified Suite for Benchmarking and Learning Language-Guided UAV Flight

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17163

作者：Donglin Yang,Xiangyu Wang,Yantong Zhong,Wenhao Zheng,Zihan Ding,Yue Liao,Hongsheng Li,Ke Xu,Si Liu

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Language-guided UAV navigation, long-horizon route planning, Language-guided UAV, leaving short-range, route planning

备注： 22 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language-guided UAV navigation has largely focused on long-horizon route planning, leaving short-range, object-relative flight maneuvers underexplored. To address this gap, we formulate Flying-on-a-Word (Flow), a task requiring a UAV to convert concise natural-language instruction, egocentric visual observation, and current state dynamics into continuous flight actions. Flow encompasses both primitive motion commands and complex object-interactive behaviors, assessing motion intent comprehension and spatial context grounding. To enable policy learning and closed-loop evaluation, we introduce UAV-Flow, a comprehensive dataset comprising 30,692 expert-piloted real-world trajectories across eight distinct motion categories, alongside 10,109 simulation trajectories in the UAV-Flow-Sim environment equipped with task-specific completion criteria. Furthermore, we develop FlowVLA, which undergoes imitation pretraining on mixed real-sim demonstrations and online post-training via Tail-Aware GRPO (TGRPO). By leveraging group reward distributions, TGRPO effectively reinforces high-reward action sequences. Evaluated on 273 held-out UAV-Flow-Sim episodes, pretrained FlowVLA achieves a mean success rate of 75.58%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.72 percentage points, which TGRPO post-training further elevates to 93.16% with superior sample efficiency. Physical UAV experiments demonstrate robust execution of object-relative commands in both indoor and outdoor real-world scenes. Together, Flow establishes a novel benchmark and learning framework for language-conditioned flight maneuvers.

18. 【2611.17146】HiVE-VQA: Hierarchical Interaction via Volumetric Embedding for Video Quality Assessment

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17146

作者：Robbie Hamilton,Fan Zhang,David Bull

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：highly important challenge, video quality assessment, user-generated video content, robust no-reference video, no-reference video quality

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027, 11 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The proliferation of user-generated video content has made accurate and robust no-reference video quality assessment (NR-VQA) a highly important challenge. Existing research increasingly relies on architecturally complex multi-branch pipelines or computationally intensive fine-tuning of single-branch backbones. In this paper, we investigate whether a single frozen video foundation model can serve as an effective NR-VQA backbone, and propose HiVE-VQA, the first framework (to our knowledge) to apply a JEPA-based video encoder to this task. HiVE-VQA extracts token grids from multiple depths of a pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder and stacks them into a hierarchical spatio-temporal volume, which a compact 3D convolutional head fuses across its layer, spatial, and temporal axes, combining low-level distortion cues from shallow layers with the semantic and temporal context captured at greater depths. The encoder remains frozen throughout, so only the head is trained: 5.2M parameters (5.7\% of the total), with no auxiliary multi-modal models and an inference cost that is invariant to video resolution. Trained solely on LSVQ and evaluated without any fine-tuning, HiVE-VQA outperforms 17 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in SROCC and PLCC on both intra-dataset (LSVQ-test, LSVQ-1080p) and cross-dataset (KoNViD-1k, LIVE-VQC) benchmarks. These results indicate that hierarchical, depth-wise fusion of features from a single frozen video foundation model provides a more effective and efficient basis for NR-VQA than task-specific multi-network fusion. Code and model checkpoints are available at this https URL.

19. 【2611.17126】STeP-C: Real-Time Neural Compression for Distributed Fiber-Optic Sensing at Scale

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17126

作者：Tingfeng Li,Ming-Fang Huang,Shaobo Han,Yue Tian

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Distributed Acoustic Sensing, Distributed fiber-optic sensing, Distributed Acoustic, creating severe challenges, Distributed fiber-optic

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027 (Applications Track). 10 pages, 9 figures, 3 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Distributed fiber-optic sensing (DFOS) technologies such as Distributed Acoustic Sensing (DAS) generate continuous, high-resolution spatio-temporal data at rates exceeding hundreds of megabytes per second, creating severe challenges for real-time storage and transmission. While neural compression methods can achieve strong rate-distortion performance on sensing data, prior approaches either require per-segment retraining incompatible with streaming, or have only been validated offline. None have been demonstrated end-to-end on a continuously streaming DAS acquisition system at scale. We present STeP-C (Spatio-Temporal Predictive Compression), a neural compression framework that decouples spatial encoding, temporal prediction, and entropy-optimized residual coding, trained end-to-end with a rate-distortion objective motivated by conditional entropy reduction. Critically, we identify entropy coding as the dominant bottleneck for streaming deployment. We address it through GPU-side symbol computation, cached probability tables, and parallel GIL-free arithmetic coding, together with FP16 inference and batched entropy coding, enabling a full real-time pipeline built around a GPU ring buffer. We validate this pipeline on a live DAS testbed, achieving a 98.85% file size reduction at 31.04 dB full-band PSNR and an average throughput of 535 MB/s, exceeding the 324 MB/s requirement for 1 kHz, 81 km DAS streams. We further show that STeP-C substantially outperforms conventional codecs at practical streaming bitrates, can be applied to unseen channel ranges within the same acquisition system without retraining, and largely maintains downstream detector consistency, with 96.62% high-confidence class agreement and 100% Jaccard similarity on detected event types.

22. 【2611.17103】Joint Null-Space Projection for Data-Free Model Merging

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17103

作者：Tian Xia

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：multi-task model requires, single multi-task model, Merging independently fine-tuned, requires no data, gradient steps

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Merging independently fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model requires no data, no labels, and no gradient steps. In practice the task vectors of different tasks interfere, and the standard remedies--averaging, magnitude pruning, sign election--operate entry by entry, ignoring that each delta induces a low-dimensional update on the hidden features it acts upon. We recast merging as a subspace interference problem. For every two-dimensional weight tensor we take the top-r right-singular subspace of each task's delta--the input directions along which that task moves the representation most--and remove from each task vector the component lying in the union of the other tasks' subspaces. The resulting Joint Null-Space Projection (JNSP) is closed-form and order-independent, needs no forward pass, and is defined entirely by the checkpoint set. On eight CLIP image-classification tasks JNSP merges ViT-B/16 experts to 89.45% average accuracy without using a single image, 11.88 points above the strongest parameter-wise training-free baseline (TIES-Merging, 77.57%), 14.22 above Task Arithmetic, and 0.98 above WUDI's data-free consensus merge (88.47%). The gain holds across three backbones on the independently trained TALL release (+10.8, +12.6, +9.1 over TIES for ViT-B/16, ViT-B/32, ViT-L/14) and replicates there at 89.20%; controls confirm the source--a single shared subspace instead of per-task ones loses 2.9 points, and a sequential variant collapses to 69.6%. On RoBERTa-large the same projection underperforms plain task arithmetic, which we report rather than hide. Code is available at this https URL.

23. 【2611.17077】SALT: State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer for Single-Temporal Cross-Dataset Structural Localization in Remote Sensing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17077

作者：Aggelos Psiris,Georgios Angelidis,Giorgos Sfikas,Maria Vakalopoulou,Thomas Lagkas,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos

备注： Accepted at the Third Workshop on Computer Vision for Geospatial Image Analysis (GeoCV), IEEE/CVF WACV 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Change detection in remote sensing conventionally relies on spatially aligned bi-temporal imagery, which may be unavailable or unreliable in practical scenarios. Recent single-temporal approaches address this limitation by learning structural evidence, such as building regions, from a labeled source dataset and transferring it to unseen target imagery using only the current image. However, adaptation to the source dataset can over-specialize pretrained features and reduce their cross-dataset generalization, a risk that this paper argues is higher when the feature adaptation is parameterized independently of the states used for prediction. To address this issue, the current study introduces SALT, a State-Aligned Low-Rank Transfer framework. SALT keeps a DINOv3 backbone frozen and learns negative and positive structural states that both score each patch and, through State-Aligned Low-Rank Adaptation (SALA), span the rank-2 subspace in which patch features are corrected. Directional State Alignment supervises these corrections to raise the evidence of positive patches and lower that of negative ones, while Geometry Preservation limits changes to the pairwise relations among pretrained patch features. Experiments on LEVIR-CD and the WHU aerial building dataset show that, at the pixel level and in both transfer directions, SALT outperforms four baselines reproduced under a common source-only protocol, improving over the strongest reproduced baselines by 9.45 and 5.79 points in pixel-level AP and by 27.69 and 14.25 points in IoU (LEVIR-CD to WHU and WHU to LEVIR-CD, respectively), with only 0.068M trainable parameters and 40.2 ms per image. In order to facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of SALT is available at this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision transformers (ViTs) serve as the backbone for countless vision tasks, including dense prediction tasks, such as segmentation and depth estimation, even though the quadratic complexity of self-attention severely limits feature resolution and induces spatial artifacts. In this work, we introduce ElasticDino, a lightweight adapter which produces high-resolution feature maps, adhering to the fine details of the input image. As a key insight, we observe the consistency of artifacts across channels, and simultaneously resolve them to match the input image structure. 29. 【2611.16907】Automated PD-L1 Tumor Proportion Scoring with Uncertainty-Guided Triage in Non-Small Cell Lung Cancer

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16907

作者：Kesi Xu,Ayesha Azam,Tanvier Kaur,Monika Kondratowicz,Kinza Asim,Brinder Chohan,Paapa Appiah-Odame,Zhao Chen,David Snead,Nasir Rajpoot

关键词：informs immunotherapy selection, Programmed death-ligand, cell lung cancer, non-small cell lung, informs immunotherapy

备注： 55 pages (29-page main text with 5 figures and 3 tables, followed by 26 pages of supplementary material)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) tumor proportion score (TPS) informs immunotherapy selection in non-small cell lung cancer. Near the 1% and 50% cutoffs, the score should also indicate when closer review is warranted. We designed a three-stage framework to follow the sequence of TPS assessment. Stage 1 identifies tumor regions and excludes macrophage-rich patches. Stage 2, a dual-branch cell counting network (D2Count-Net), counts PD-L1-positive and PD-L1-negative tumor cells in these regions to calculate TPS. Stage 3, TPS-Guard, an uncertainty-guided triage mechanism, reports model uncertainty as the disagreement among independently trained models, refers the most uncertain slides, and flags scores near the 1% or 50% cutoff when model estimates disagree across that cutoff. Flags state the reason for referral or alert the pathologist to possible scoring errors on auto-reported slides. In a retrospective, single-center study, 154 whole-slide images were scored by three pathologists, and the model and each pathologist were compared in turn with the mean of the other two. On the 114 slides unseen in training, model mean absolute error (MAE) was 6.96 percentage points, within the range of the three pathologists (4.24 to 8.29), and model uncertainty correlated with absolute TPS error (Spearman rho = 0.77) and inter-pathologist disagreement (rho = 0.71). After referral of the 15% most uncertain slides, model and pathologist MAE both decreased. Applied without retraining to an external, publicly available dataset, TPS-Guard still directed review to higher-error cases, and referring more uncertain cases raised sensitivity at the 50% cutoff. TPS-Guard provides reasons for reviewing individual automated scores and identifies slides that also produce greater disagreement among pathologists. Prospective reader studies should assess TPS-Guard's effect on TPS reporting.

32. 【2611.16861】Visual Cipher Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Visual Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16861

作者：Deyao Hong,Jiayi Jiang,Yuchun Chen,Yixuan Shi,Wenyi Li,Zheng Jiang,Chengxing Xie,Mingyu Su,Qingle Liu,Mingju Gao,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Huan-ang Gao

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:While language-model agents excel at long-horizon coding and terminal tasks, this success relies on two conditions: explicit text specifications and immediate execution feedback. However, neither condition holds in real-world problems where critical information exists only in images, leaving it unclear whether long-horizon capability transfers from text to visual reasoning. In such visual settings, because rules and clues resist text transcription and lack test-case feedback, agents must repeatedly inspect images, simulate spatial transformations, and autonomously cross-check clues. Despite this challenge, existing evaluations fail to capture such capability: visual reasoning benchmarks largely reduce tasks to single-turn, short-chain question answering, whereas agent benchmarks focus on text-based environments or interactive states that permit trial-and-error. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual Cipher Bench, a collection of 40 original visual puzzles across four categories: spatial manipulation and simulation, logical constraints and planning, rule induction, and knowledge-based decoding. Each puzzle provides only one to three black-and-white line drawings and a minimal submission instruction, requiring agents to discover the underlying solving mechanism entirely from the images. Across 29 reasoning-effort configurations of eight frontier models, even the strongest model, GPT-6 Astra (max), achieves only 26.7% accuracy, while five of the eight models score below 1%. Yet on a ten-task subset, providing a single non-spoiler mechanism hint boosts accuracy from 7.8% to 55.6%. This sharp contrast shows that the primary bottleneck of current agents lies not in executing multi-step plans, but in autonomously discovering rules from visual evidence. Visual Cipher Bench thus provides a rigorous benchmark for measuring long-horizon visual reasoning in agents.

These results support the use of learned geometric priors within a scene-level reconstruction pipeline.

36. 【2611.16764】Efficient Periocular Recognition through Model Scaling and Cross Spectral VIS NIR Verification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16764

作者：Fabian Stockhard,Juan E. Tapia,Christoph Busch

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： Munuscript In revision Process, abstract limited to 1920 ch

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Periocular recognition offers an efficient alternative to full-face verification when facial regions are occluded or image resolution is limited. We present AdaEye, a systematic study of input and backbone scaling for periocular verification in visible-spectrum (VIS) and cross-spectral (VIS/NIR) settings. Using an AdaFace-style framework, we compare full-face, masked-face, and dedicated 80x40 periocular inputs while varying backbone width and embedding dimensionality. Across four VIS/VIS and VIS/NIR protocols, we assess verification accuracy, computational cost, inference throughput, training memory, template size, and training stability. Direct periocular processing reduces recognition cost from 6.30 to 1.64 GMACs per image and increases measured throughput by approximately threefold. Its effectiveness depends on the evaluation protocol: periocular models perform better on native UFPR periocular images, whereas face-based protocols favor retaining more input information. Moderate backbone width reductions maintain comparable mean verification performance on CASIA and ND-NIVL while reducing computation. Embedding compression decreases template-storage requirements, primarily benefiting deployment. Multi-seed experiments reveal that some configurations require numerical stabilization, particularly when embedding and backbone reductions are combined. These findings support selecting compact periocular architectures according to the target protocol and deployment constraints, balancing accuracy, efficiency, storage, and robustness.

40. 【2611.16742】Fog-FWL: A Large-Scale Full-Waveform LiDAR Dataset for Recovering Fog-Hidden Objects

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16742

作者：Kazuma Ikeda,Kotaro Oishi,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Takahiro Kado,Ibuki Fujioka,Taro Beppu,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：autonomous driving, highly vulnerable, real foggy FWL, core sensor, FWL

备注： 6 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LiDAR is a core sensor for autonomous driving, yet it is highly vulnerable to fog. Fog scattering produces strong backscatter near the sensor and attenuates reflections from objects behind it, so weak object returns are often discarded by the peak detection that converts received signals into point clouds. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL), which records the received optical signal before peak detection, retains these weak returns, but the lack of suitable data has prevented research on recovering fog-hidden objects from FWL. We present Fog-FWL, a dataset foundation for this task that consists of a real-world benchmark, Fog-FWL-Real, and a large-scale synthetic training set, Fog-FWL-Sim. Fog-FWL-Real contains 7,527 real foggy FWL frames from 8 scenes and 58 sequences, which is 579x larger than the only existing real foggy FWL data, together with histogram-level Object/Fog/Noise labels, 10,719 3D bounding boxes, and three difficulty levels defined by the attenuation of object reflections. Fog-FWL-Sim contains 12,000 frames generated by our physics-based simulator, Fog-FWL-Simulator, which integrates spatially heterogeneous fog density fields, time-resolved light transport including multiple scattering, and a photon-counting SPAD receiver model. The two subsets share the same sensor format and annotation protocol, enabling a strict sim-to-real evaluation in which no real waveforms are used for training. Our analysis shows that, under the hardest condition, object returns are included in the three strongest peaks for only 34.9% of object rays, underscoring the need for waveform-level recovery. Fog-FWL-Real, Fog-FWL-Sim, and the Fog-FWL-Simulator will be publicly released to facilitate research on fog-robust LiDAR perception.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16727

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Adapting general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to Autonomous Driving (AD) is thought to require heavy 3D spatial priors or intrusive architectural overhauls to compensate for a lack of spatial grounding. In this work, we present a counterintuitive pilot study: off-the-shelf general VLMs already exhibit remarkable adaptability across diverse driving tasks, while precise metric depth remains the primary missing link, a bottleneck readily addressable via onboard sensors and depth foundation models. Driven by automatically constructed (image, depth, QA pair) triplets, our framework operates via a unified tri-stream training pipeline: an unfrozen primary VLM stream to adapt to driving tasks, an auxiliary depth stream that guides the visual backbone to implicitly imagine 3D scene geometry via explicit geometric constraints, and a frozen twin VLM stream employing logit-level KL distillation to prevent semantic drift. Crucially, the auxiliary depth branch is discarded during deployment, ensuring the enhanced geometric awareness comes with zero inference overhead. We evaluate our framework across four major benchmarks targeting four diverse capabilities spanning 3D spatial perception, driving-oriented language reasoning, end-to-end planning, and general multimodal understanding. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art driving VLMs, rivals domain-specific expert models in 3D perception, and successfully retains its original general reasoning capabilities. This demonstrates that Triplet Incremental Training offers a unified, deployment-ready paradigm for transforming general foundation models into robust driving intelligences.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image-to-3D generative models now produce asset-quality textured meshes, but at minutes per asset they remain slow to iterate with and costly to deploy. Prior work has mostly accelerated the sampling of the networks, and where it also optimizes the mesh processing, it trains its own generators. We instead start from a publicly released pipeline and ask how much of its end-to-end time can be saved. Profiling the public Pixal3D pipeline shows that its latency is dominated by what happens after the networks: the four rectified flows take 14% of the wall-clock time and mesh post-processing takes 85%, most of it in CPU-based UV unwrapping. We therefore first rebuild the mesh post-processing on the GPU and take it off the critical path, then cut the per-evaluation overhead of the flows and distill them from 76 to 8 network evaluations. The rebuilt post-processing alone also speeds up TRELLIS.2, which uses the same export code, by 6.7$\times$ without a detectable change in the evaluated metrics.

45. 【2611.16650】Learning the Missing Derivative: Neural Multiple-Scattering Operators for Inverse Volume Rendering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16650

作者：Sipeng Chen,Shibo Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：photographs requires modeling, requires modeling multiple, Reconstructing optically thick, modeling multiple scattering, Reconstructing optically

备注： 21 pages, 11 figures, 18 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reconstructing optically thick media from photographs requires modeling multiple scattering and its response to changes in extinction. Frozen-source tomography discards this derivative; differentiable path tracing estimates it through expensive, noisy sampling. We instead predict the multiple-scattering field with a neural operator and differentiate through it during inverse rendering, while computing transmittance and single scattering directly. A further challenge arises during optimization: an operator that predicts the field accurately on held-out media can still produce poor derivatives at the density states visited by the optimizer. We address this by fine-tuning on states generated during inversions driven by the operator, using Monte Carlo scattering-field supervision. On held-out optically thick clouds, our method improves relighting over frozen-source tomography at equal time and outperforms differentiable path tracing given substantially more time. Training on inversion trajectories also improves derivative alignment at visited states and reduces optimization drift.

46. 【2611.16642】Dataset Distillation via Drifting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16642

作者：George Cazenavette,Angelina Quan,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba,Vincent Sitzmann

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：downstream tasks, concerns the learning, effective replacement, dataset distillation concerns, large real dataset

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Project Page: [this https URL](https://drifting-distillation.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The task of dataset distillation concerns the learning of a small set of synthetic samples that can serve as an effective replacement for a large real dataset when used for downstream tasks. While many solutions have been proposed since the problem's inception, most of them primarily seek to optimize the distilled data with respect to a classifier. In this work, we propose a new distillation method that learns a set of samples such that they directly approximate the per-class data distribution. Specifically, we "drift" our distilled data in the latent space of a feature extractor such that they move both toward the real data and away from each other without using a classification objective. We find that our drifted data cover the real distribution significantly better than existing methods while still serving as powerful training samples for classification. Furthermore, we showcase our method's use as a visualization tool for better interpreting models and extend it beyond images to other modalities. Our work advances the frontier of dataset distillation, offering a novel synthesis method and proposing distribution-based metrics as a complementary evaluation. More broadly, our findings help address the growing need to understand both data and models by uncovering more about how they interact.

47. 【2611.16639】Asymmetric Multimodal Learning with Decomposed JEPAs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16639

作者：Yusuf Shihata

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：building multimodal representations, Alignment Redundancy Bound, standard objective, alignment, multimodal representations

备注： 15 pages, 12 tables, 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-modal alignment is the standard objective for building multimodal representations. We show that alignment as a \emph{sole} objective is lossy wherever modalities are informationally asymmetric---where neither can express what the other carries. Visual question answering is an obvious case: a question is a selective relational operator probing a dense visual field whose fine-grained features no query recovers, so what alignment preserves is not what the task requires. We prove an \textbf{Alignment Redundancy Bound}: alignment-only Dirichlet energy minimization retains at most the redundant information atom $\Red(Z; X_1, X_2)$, annihilating private modality-specific structure $\Unq(Z; X_m)$ and cross-modal synergy $\Syn(Z; X_1, X_2)$. We introduce \textbf{Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)}, which factorizes latents into an aligned shared core and private modality-specific fibers. The shared core retains most of the structure alignment targets; the private fibers recover what alignment discards, via contextual masked prediction and asymmetric cross-subspace independence. Under information asymmetry, alignment-only training undergoes catastrophic semantic collapse on compositional reasoning benchmarks, surviving only on binary queries. D-JEPA averts this collapse, substantially improving downstream accuracy over both alignment-only and alignment-with-reconstruction baselines, and restoring a positive multimodal synergy gap in place of the baseline's inverted gap. Information-theoretic audits confirm that D-JEPA recovers most of the mutual information available from a frozen backbone, and causal fiber-swap interventions demonstrate that the recovered information is causally localized in the private fibers.

48. 【2611.16630】When Chekhov's Gun Fires Anyway: Over-Realization in Text-to-Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16630

作者：Jiahao Huang,Kon Woo Kim,Song-Duo Ma,Yuka Kitamura,Felix Wagner,Yunshan Huang,Sunisth Kumar,Xanh Ho,Yun-Ang Wu,Zhijie Chen,Yunjia Xu,Junfeng Jiang,Zefan Yu,Fei Cheng,Akiko Aizawa

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In text-to-image generation, a target that the prompt mentions often appears in the generated image in a way the prompt does not call for. We call this failure over-realization. Mental-spaces theory points to the situations where it can happen: under a negation, inside what a character believes or imagines, as the vehicle of a figurative comparison, or behind a viewpoint that hides the target. Prior work has studied only negation in depth, and the conventional alignment and preference metrics do not penalize the failure. We build OverReal to measure it: OverReal-Gen, 698 prompts covering the four situations, on which we measure each generator's over-realization rate; and OverReal-Det, 2,841 generated images human-labeled for whether and how the target is over-realized, on which we verify the automatic judge that produces this rate. Our evaluation of eight text-to-image systems reveals that all of them over-realize, and that conventional metrics such as CLIPScore reward the failure rather than penalize it. Prompt expansion only partly mitigates the failure. Inside the generator, the diffusion backbone attends to a mentioned target almost as much as to a requested one. The cue words that should hold the target back receive less attention than ordinary words.

49. 【2611.16628】Safety as a Budget: Calibrating the Control Channel of Constrained GRPO for Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16628

作者：Bo Han

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：harmful image-text requests, decline harmful image-text, reward weights found, teach vision-language models, Reinforcement learning

备注： 10 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning can teach vision-language models (VLMs) to decline harmful image-text requests, but how safe the result becomes is set indirectly, through reward weights found by trial runs. Stating safety as budgets on the rate of harmful assistance and the rate of over-refusal, enforced by Lagrange multipliers inside GRPO with an off-the-shelf scorer as the cost, is the natural fix, and it fails twice. The multiplier compares the scorer's mean with the budget, and scorers built for ranking get the mean wrong: a fixed-point analysis shows that the mean bias, not the budget, then decides whether the multiplier settles, saturates or switches off. And standard GRPO standardizes the penalized reward within each group, which cancels the multiplier before it reaches the gradient. Two-channel constrained GRPO repairs both: the base reward keeps the raw score for the within-group comparison, the penalty and the constraint estimate pass through a monotone map fitted on judge-labeled training responses, and the advantage keeps the multiplier. On a Qwen3-VL-2B policy with a training-free readout of an open 4B model as the scorer, held-out harm on 1,037 adversarial prompts falls from 8.9% under standard GRPO to 4.8%, lower in every seed, where the uncalibrated budget gives 7.6%; over-refusal stays at most 2% on 400 benign queries and MMBench is unchanged. The gain is largest where the intent sits in the image (10.1% - 5.1%) and grows on the unseen HADES benchmark (28.9% - 12.8%). Order-preserving distortions of the scores reproduce each predicted controller state in every seed, and the results hold under a map fitted on another judge's labels, an image-reading judge and a human audit.

Abstract:The spatial resolution of neutron images is of great importance for material defect diagnosis, structural characterization, and nondestructive testing. 关键词：computational capacity, classification in resource-constrained, resource-constrained settings, settings must contend, contend with limited

备注： 17 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical image classification in resource-constrained settings must contend with limited annotations and computational capacity. We evaluate four ZACH-ViT variants with linear, Euclidean prototype, Poincare and spherical heads alongside two compact multiple-instance-learning baselines. All models are trained from scratch with fewer than 260,000 parameters across seven MedMNIST+ datasets and three external benchmarks (PCam, ISIC2017 and Shenzhen tuberculosis radiographs). The low-data protocol uses 50 training images per class and five seeds, with validation-selected checkpoints at 23 and 47 epochs; a full-data reference uses one seed. Compact-TransMIL achieves the highest ten-dataset mean test Macro-F1 at 23 epochs (53.34%) and with full data (73.93%). At 47 epochs, ZACH-Linear narrowly leads (57.76% versus 57.54% for Compact-TransMIL). Extending training improves Macro-F1 in 239 of 300 paired comparisons. On ISIC2017, hZACH-Spherical has the highest 47-epoch low-data Macro-F1 (44.09% with SD 4.09%), whereas ZACH-EuclideanProto leads with full data (51.27%). We additionally conduct 40 CPU-only training runs on ISIC2017 and Shenzhen using four CPU threads; model-only single-image inference requires approximately 4-9 ms, while image decoding and preprocessing substantially increase end-to-end latency. The results demonstrate the feasibility of CPU-only training on the tested platform and the task- and regime-dependence of compact medical imaging under strict resource constraints.

52. 【2611.16583】ReFineR: Single image to dense point cloud registration at pixel level with point splatting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16583

作者：Ugo Insalaco,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud,Pierre Dellenbach

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Light Detection, Detection and Ranging, LiDAR sensors provide, provide complementary observations, sensors provide accurate

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cameras and LiDARs (Light Detection and Ranging) provide complementary observations of the same scene: cameras capture rich appearance cues while LiDAR sensors provide accurate geometric measurements. In this work, we address the problem of fine image-to-point cloud registration from a single image and a roughly estimated camera pose. On robotic platforms, residual reprojection errors of several pixels are commonly observed even after calibration due to temporal desynchronization, mechanical vibrations, or inaccuracies in point cloud reconstruction. Such errors can lead to decimeter-level geometric discrepancies at moderate distances and significantly affect multiple downstream tasks, such as dense cross-modal supervision, texture mapping, and semantic label transfer. To address this challenge, we introduce ReFineR: an offline framework to finely register individual images to pre-reconstructed point clouds. ReFineR builds upon differentiable point splatting rasterization and a smooth Gradient Orientation Measure (GOM) to iteratively refine the camera pose through analysis-by-synthesis optimization. We evaluated the method on a curated dataset of manually calibrated images collected from heterogeneous data sources. Furthermore, we show that an automatic point cloud rescale factor can be estimated prior to optimization, enabling robust refinement across a wide range of scenarios using a single scale-invariant formulation. The project page can be found at this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

Abstract:Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable future prediction in latent space without pixel reconstruction. However, latent predictive consistency alone does not establish the relative driving quality of candidate plans. We present ActWorld-JEPA, a joint-embedding formulation for candidate-based driving. Where Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA) uses global and step-wise trajectory intentions to guide recurrent latent prediction, then combines the predicted future with the current scene through gated cross-attention; Relational Residual Reranking ($R^3$) models agreement and discrepancy between plan and future representations under planning supervision, yielding bounded, gated residual corrections to the base planning scores. This work provides an effective path from JEPA's predictive representations to driving decisions informed by anticipated consequences.

Abstract:Face presentation attack detection requires representations that distinguish physical spoofing cues across changes in acquisition devices, subjects, and attack media. On SiW-Mv2, it achieves 3.36\% mean HTER across the held-out attack categories. Controlled loss ablations examine the contributions of latent prediction, masked classification, and prediction consistency. The results show that context-predictive adaptation can improve cross-dataset PAD performance, although the gains vary with the training sources and evaluation setting.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16517

备注： Technical Report, 31 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present UniSpark-9B, a unified multimodal model that investigates hybrid attention for visual understanding and generation. Built on a Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts backbone with approximately 9B parameters in each branch, UniSpark combines full attention for explicit token interactions with GatedDeltaNet linear attention for compact context propagation. Autoregressive text prediction and visual flow matching jointly support visual question answering, text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and interleaved text-image prediction. In interleaved generation, noisy visual targets are isolated from subsequent predictions, while clean visual representations provide context for later outputs. A blockwise protocol enforces this distinction through attention masks in full-attention layers and recurrent-state retention rules in linear-attention layers. The selected training sources contain 159.7M generation samples, 213.4M understanding samples, and 624.8K interleaved sequences. Together, these results demonstrate that hybrid attention provides an effective backbone for unified multimodal understanding and generation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Low-light image and video enhancement has received increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing enhancement methods are developed and evaluated on still images, while progress in low-light video enhancement remains limited by the lack of real-world paired video datasets. Acquiring paired low-light and normal-light video sequences is challenging because it requires spatially and temporally aligned captures while avoiding motion blur, exposure mismatch, and correspondence errors in dynamic scenes. To address this gap, we introduce PiLLVD, the Pi-based Low-Light Video Dataset, a paired low-light video dataset captured using an accessible Raspberry Pi-based multi-camera system with off-the-shelf cameras. PiLLVD provides paired low-light and aligned normal-light reference sequences for dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes, including diverse human and object motion under varying illumination conditions. Unlike existing datasets that are often image-based, static, synthetic, or limited to simple motion patterns, PiLLVD focuses on motion-rich scenes where frame-to-frame stability and motion-related artefacts are important for evaluation. In addition to presenting the dataset and acquisition pipeline, we benchmark representative pre-trained low-light video enhancement methods on PiLLVD under a zero-shot setting. The results show that existing methods do not generalise uniformly across the proposed sequences, highlighting the challenges posed by the proposed dataset and its value for both benchmarking existing methods and supporting the development of future low-light video enhancement algorithms.

59. 【2611.16464】From State of THE ART to Human Prediction Algorithms

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16464

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Human curiosity and longing for the future have driven human progress from ancient times to the present. With the advancement of AI technology, human prediction algorithms have also drawn increasing attention from researchers. However, as AI technology continues to advance, we observe a counterintuitive phenomenon. Take human trajectory prediction algorithms as an example. Although models' quantitative prediction performance has improved, yielding one state-of-the-art model after another, the consideration of human behaviors, future thoughts, and social interactions themselves presents a gradually diminishing trend in the construction of these prediction algorithms, buried beneath numbers and metrics. In this manuscript, taking interactive art and human trajectory prediction algorithms as examples, we attempt to adopt a different analytical perspective to analyze the structural and developmental commonalities between these two fields. By examining specific interactive artworks and prediction algorithms, we aim to explore how human intuitions and art are associated with AI algorithms through three aspects, including perception, interaction, and timing, thereby providing new insights for the understanding and further research in this domain. We also observe an interesting phenomenon that certain art-inspired prediction algorithms can actually deduce intriguing patterns of human behaviors from the data, offering new potential avenues for understanding the relationship between human activities, state of The Art, and AI algorithms.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16452

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) reason effectively about video content, but whether they perceive its event boundaries in a manner consistent with humans remains unclear. We first investigate this through Generic Event Boundary Detection (GEBD), which identifies event boundaries naturally perceived by humans in continuous video, without relying on predefined event categories. On Kinetics-GEBD dataset, LVLMs miss many boundaries when predicting directly from frames, but effectively refine candidates proposed by an unsupervised discovery method, achieving state-of-the-art unsupervised GEBD performance. We extend this discovery-refinement approach to hour-long egocentric video, where boundary discovery requires a broader semantic scale and whole-video LVLM refinement becomes impractical. We address these challenges through coarse-to-fine temporal discovery and localized LVLM accept/reject judgments. Human evaluation shows that the LVLM's agreement with group consensus falls within the range observed among human annotators, with disagreements concentrated on low-consensus cases. Using this pipeline, we construct EgoBoundary, a benchmark for event boundary detection in hour-scale egocentric video. Representative supervised and unsupervised methods degrade substantially on EgoBoundary, highlighting the challenge of long-horizon event boundary detection.

61. 【2611.16451】HIFC-IQA: Train-Free Cross-Domain Image Quality Assessment via Dual-Process Cognition

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16451

作者：Yu Li,Zhengran Shen,Puchao Zhou,Yukang Ding,Yachun Mi,Boyuan Yang,Xi Zhai,Shaohui Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Image Quality Assessment, No-Reference Image Quality, complex real-world degradations, real-world degradations introduces, critical domain shift

备注： Accepted by NeurIPS2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The gap between controlled synthetic distortions and complex real-world degradations introduces a critical domain shift challenge for No-Reference Image Quality Assessment. Although Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) approaches attempt to address this issue, they remain constrained by substantial retraining overhead and cumulative error propagation inherent in iterative pseudo-labeling. Inspired by the dual-process theory of human cognition for remarkable generalization ability, we propose Hierarchical Intuitive and Fuzzy Consensus (HIFC), a completely train-free Cross-Domain IQA framework. HIFC employs asymmetric perceptual decomposition to disentangle semantic content from distortion-sensitive representations. The final quality score is fused through the synergy of two complementary branches: a memory consensus branch that retrieves a stable Fuzzy Quality Centroid from a synthetic reference gallery, and an intuitive branch that captures aesthetic polarization via zero-shot vision-language alignment. Extensive experiments show that HIFC achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, attaining the highest average SRCC and PLCC among competing UDA methods. Most notably, in the challenging synthetic-to-authentic cross-domain setting, HIFC achieves a substantial performance margin over competing methods, demonstrating remarkable robustness and generalization.

Abstract:Multimodal dataset distillation (MDD) aims to reduce the cost of vision-language pre-training by synthesizing compact image-text datasets. Although recent feature-matching methods have improved distillation efficiency, synthetic data may become specialized to the model representations encountered during optimization, limiting generalization to unseen model states. We propose Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation (MDA-DD), which leverages supervision from a dynamic staggered queue of models at different training stages. 67. 【2611.16413】RICapBench: A Fine-Grained Visual Understanding Benchmark Dataset for Vision-Language Models in Remote Sensing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16413

作者：Xue Wang,Yubin Xu,Kun Tan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Advances in vision-language, supported geospatial decision-making, sensing image analysis, remote sensing, instance localization

备注： 13 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have improved remote sensing image analysis and supported geospatial decision-making. However, fine-grained understanding of remote sensing imagery remains challenging for VLMs. Large variations in object scale, complex geospatial relations among instances, and limited visual detail hinder precise instance localization, visible attribute identification, and spatial relation understanding, potentially leading to descriptions that lack visual grounding. Existing datasets and benchmarks insufficiently assess end-to-end instance localization and captioning without target-specific textual cues. To support this evaluation, we introduce RICapBench, a dataset and benchmark that unifies instance localization and fine-grained captioning in remote sensing. RICapBench contains 245,466 instance-level annotations and 498,005 spatial relation tuples spanning general remote sensing, UAV remote sensing, and dense small-object scenarios. Each annotation pairs an object's oriented bounding box with a natural language description of its visible attributes and spatial relations to surrounding instances and environmental elements. LocateAnything achieves the highest RICScore of 31.68, but scores lower on spatial relation captioning than other task-fine-tuned open-source VLMs. Code and dataset are available at this https URL.

Abstract:On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective post-training approach to improving fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models by amortizing costly inference-time zooming operations into a single-pass recipe. By extracting more effective supervision from each on-policy trajectory, BlinkOPD achieves state-of-the-art average performance across six fine-grained benchmarks using only 500 training images (12x less data) than existing OPD methods. Abstract:An assistant watching a worker can prepare for an emerging need before the worker is ready to receive this http URL the need evolves, the assistant must maintain preparation until a response is due, or withdraw it if the need disappears. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16382

作者：Shenghai Wu,Yue Lin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Multimedia (cs.MM)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Universal multi-modal fusion often fails not because modalities are insufficiently aligned, but because the fusion process allocates information poorly: redundant evidence is repeatedly amplified, modality-unique evidence is over-smoothed during alignment, and task-dependent synergistic evidence is insufficiently activated. We present RADIANT (Redundancy-Aware Dynamic Information Allocation Network), a task-general framework that organizes fusion around three complementary roles: suppressing redundant cross-modal overlap, preserving modality-unique evidence, and composing task-conditioned synergy through cross-modal interaction. Specifically, stable matrix-exponential projections are used to reduce interference among shared and modality-unique streams, while task memory with hard positive updates selects which synergistic interactions should be activated for the current fusion regime. Extensive experiments across six heterogeneous fusion tasks show consistent improvements over the compared methods. On FLIR, RADIANT improves downstream detection mAP@0.5 from 91.2% to 96.8%, a gain of 5.6 percentage points (6.1% relative) over GIF. RADIANT also exhibits strong direct cross-dataset transfer, with further gains from inference-time task-memory adaptation while keeping all network weights frozen. Additional ablations and sensitivity studies support the robustness of the allocation mechanism.

76. 【2611.16310】Selective Generative Restoration for Video Deraining via Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16310

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video deraining is challenging due to rain streaks, occlusions, and atmospheric effects. Existing methods mainly follow a deterministic restoration paradigm, assuming that clean scenes can be uniquely recovered from rainy observations. However, rain degradation is spatially heterogeneous. Reliable regions require faithful reconstruction, whereas severely occluded regions contain ambiguous content that require stronger prior-driven inference. Therefore, conventional deterministic restoration often produces over-smoothed results, while global generative restoration may introduce unnecessary hallucinations. We introduce a Selective Generative Restoration paradigm for video deraining, where generative priors are adaptively activated according to restoration uncertainty. Based on this principle, we propose Selective Generative Video Deraining (SGVD), an uncertainty-aware diffusion framework that enables adaptive fidelity preservation and generative recovery. Specifically, SGVD exploits stochastic diversity between dual one-step diffusion predictions to estimate region-level restoration uncertainty, which determines where observation fidelity should be preserved and where generative inference should be activated. Furthermore, an anchor-guided trajectory consistency mechanism is proposed to regularize stochastic diffusion evolution across frames, reducing temporal generation drift and improving video coherence. To achieve practical efficiency, SGVD adopts one-step diffusion inference while maintaining uncertainty-aware generative capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGVD achieves superior restoration performance, perceptual quality, and temporal consistency compared with state-of-the-art video deraining methods.

80. 【2611.16236】ATune: Unsupervised Photo Retouching Through Image-Editor Controls

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16236

作者：Divyanshu Goyal,Gabriel Ruiz,Amir Meisami

备注： 27 pages, 12 figures, 18 tables. Main paper 8 pages plus supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Photo retouching models are trained on pairs of original and retouched photographs, usually edited by experts. Expert edits are costly, so the two main public sets, FiveK and PPR10K, hold only 16,161 originals. Good photographs on their own are far easier to find: commercial stock collections and free ones such as Pixabay and Unsplash hold millions. We learn retouching from them alone: we damage each photograph the way a camera or a careless edit would, which gives a worse and a better version of the same photograph. From these pairs, our model, ATune, learns how 13 measured attributes, such as brightness, contrast and sharpness, should change to raise a photograph's quality. The edit is declarative: ATune sets target values for the attributes, and a simple search adjusts the sliders of an out-of-the-box photo editor until the photograph reaches them. Every edit is a list of slider values a person can read and adjust. The same model can drive any editor that makes those changes; we test RawTherapee and darktable. We train on 10M photographs from a commercial stock collection and test on FiveK, PPR10K and SPAQ. Nine quality judges score every edit, and in a user study 157 human raters made 9,253 choices between our edit and another method's edit of the same photograph. When Claude Opus 5 and GPT-6 Astra edit the same photographs in the same editor, raters prefer our edits to GPT-6 Astra's (54.3%) and rate them even with Claude's (50.5%). We also compare with supervised retouchers on their home ground: NeurOP on FiveK and AdaInt on PPR10K, each trained on that set's own expert edits. Raters chose our edits in 46.3% of those comparisons. ATune, a 54M-parameter model trained without a single expert edit, now matches frontier models at setting an editor's sliders.

81. 【2611.16224】Disentangled Embedding Optimization for Diffusion-Based Dataset Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16224

作者：Hongcheng Li,Yucan Zhou,Yibin Wang,Xiaoyan Gu,Bo Li,Weiping Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：enable efficient model, efficient model training, Dataset distillation aims, compress large-scale datasets, compact synthetic subsets

备注： 10 pages, 7 figures. Accepted at the 34th ACM International Conference on Multimedia (ACM MM 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dataset distillation aims to compress large-scale datasets into compact synthetic subsets that enable efficient model training with comparable performance to the original data. Recent advances show that diffusion models are highly effective for this task due to their strong generative capability. Existing diffusion-based approaches typically perform class-wise clustering and use the obtained cluster prototypes as diffusion inputs to generate distilled samples. However, the uniformly distributed original samples in a category inevitably lead to entanglement near cluster boundaries, pulling the cluster prototypes closer and reducing the inter-cluster separation. Consequently, the diffusion model receives overly similar latent inputs, resulting in synthetic data with limited diversity. To address this issue, we propose Disentangled Embedding Optimization (DEO), a simple yet effective framework that replaces static clustering with trainable embeddings. These embeddings are optimized to match the Gaussian statistics (i.e., mean and covariance) of the original data distribution, while a diversity regularizer is designed to encourage separation among these embeddings to improve the intra-class variability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed DEO can generate more diversified and representative distilled data than existing cluster-based diffusion methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and superior generalization across multiple datasets.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16217

作者：Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Harold Haodong Chen,Weilin Ruan,Chenfei Liao,Liuzhou Zhang,Disen Lan,Xiaogang Xu,Qi Chen,Ser-Nam Lim

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal coding agents decouple 4D world construction from visual generation, repurposing video models from world generators toward generative renderers. This division of labor raises a fundamental question: if code builds the world, can video models just render it? A reliable generative renderer hinges on two complementary criteria: (i) world preservation, retaining prescribed 4D kinematics and camera paths; and (ii) visual realization, persistently binding text-specified target attributes over time. To evaluate this capability, we introduce WorldDraft, a benchmark of $432$ strictly paired test cases across $48$ executable 4D world families spanning six dynamic challenge categories. Under a $3 \times 3$ factorial design, WorldDraft orthogonally crosses draft granularity (abstract proxy to previs) and appearance alignment (neutral to conflicting) under identical dynamics. Extensive evaluations across $12$ representative models reveal that: (I) world preservation and visual realization remain in stark operational tension; (II) richer draft scaffolding anchors structure but paradoxically impairs appearance fidelity; and (III) conflicting source cues trigger severe source leakage (up to $64\%$), exposing fragile cross-modal disentanglement. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose WorldDraft-Primer, a plug-and-play visual prompting strategy that consistently improves both objectives across frozen video renderers. Together, WorldDraft and WorldDraft-Primer lay a foundation for bridging programmatic 4D simulation with video generation, advancing physically grounded generative rendering.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LiDAR point clouds exhibit scan-induced spatial regularities that are informative for geometry compression. Existing scan-aware approaches often exploit these regularities by transforming coded geometry into spherical or beam-aligned coordinate systems. However, this couples acquisition priors with geometry representation, altering spatial partitioning and quantization, and potentially preventing exact recovery of the original Cartesian geometry. 备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Latent 3D representations have significantly expanded the capabilities of modern shape modeling, enabling compact encoding of 3D scenes and supporting powerful 3D generative models. Despite this progress, current representations struggle to capture the fine details of complex objects, fundamentally limited by the capacity of their latents. However, scaling latent capacity at training time is computationally expensive and often infeasible under memory limits, and naively increasing the number of latents at inference fails to make effective use of the added capacity. In this paper, we address both challenges with \textit{split-and-scale}, a method that effectively expands latent capacity at inference while keeping training cost tractable. Our key observation is that by representing 3D scenes as continuous functions of 3D coordinates, a single pretrained tokenizer can be applied at inference to arbitrarily small sub-regions of a scene, allocating the same latent capacity to a smaller spatial extent and thus capturing far higher detail. We validate this insight by conducting extensive ablations to study the trade-offs between compute, memory, latent capacity, and reconstruction quality, demonstrating our inference-time scaling method achieves quality competitive with much larger models that exceed single-GPU memory limits. We further demonstrate that split-and-scale supports high-quality generative modeling, training an autoregressive 3D generator that performs competitively with state-of-the-art baselines.

86. 【2611.16061】Pothole Risk Assessment with Geometrically Grounded Vision Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16061

作者：Abdalmalek Aburaddaha,Zaid A. El Shair,Ali Al-Bustami,Samir A. Rawashdeh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Potholes impose billions, annual vehicle damage, impose billions, billions of dollars, dollars in annual

备注： 22 pages, 8 figures, 17 tables. Submitted to IEEE Access. Code and data: [this https URL](https://github.com/malekaburaddaha/Pothole_Assessment_Pipeline)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Potholes impose billions of dollars in annual vehicle damage, yet detectors report only where potholes are, not how severe they are or what to do. Judging severity from a single RGB image is ill-posed, and ungrounded vision-language model (VLM) assessments tend to be over-conservative. We present a five-stage modular framework for the offline assessment of already-detected potholes that combines geometric measurements with VLM reasoning: perspective transformation, zero-shot SAM segmentation, geometric feature extraction, Gemini 2.5 Pro severity classification with composite risk scoring, and natural-language warning generation. We evaluate it on 3,092 potholes in 1,119 dashcam scenes against independent references. Severity agreement with a prediction-free three-annotator panel is weak (Cohen's $\kappa = 0.139$, 95% CI [0.00, 0.28]; $n = 101$) and far below the annotators' agreement with one another ($\kappa = 0.605$); against physically measured depth on an independent RGB-D dataset, it is also weak ($\kappa = 0.098$), although the predicted classes preserve the order of measured depths. The severity classes are therefore perceived visual categories, not depth measurements. Geometric grounding does change the warnings: relative to an image-only baseline, it moves 26.7% of scenes out of the high-urgency tier and raises critical-tier assignments by 109% (Stuart-Maxwell $p 10^{-15}$), and in a blind study, three raters preferred the grounded warnings 3.5:1. A six-arm prompt ablation attributes the hybrid prompt's rule adherence to the per-pothole measurements rather than to the rubric or worked examples. At USD 0.105 per scene, it suits large-scale offline infrastructure assessment; its maneuver suggestions are advisory research outputs, not validated vehicle guidance.

Abstract:Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16012

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://topoego.github.io)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Topo4DGS jointly models part-level motion and time-varying surface support by assigning independent rigid transformations to interacting parts and dynamically activating or deactivating Gaussians as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 4DGS approaches across topological changes, especially on post-event frames. Code and data will be made publicly available.

89. 【2611.16010】Iris-Z: Scaling One Generative Model Past Specialist Geometry Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16010

作者：Alejandro Cañada,Chema Garabito

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

备注： 14 pages, 9 figures, 6 tables. Code and models: [this https URL](https://github.com/speridlabs/iris-z-sdk)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language moved from many task-specific models to one unified model: a single generative model with a single generative training task that handles all of them within the same backbone, and keeps improving with data, parameters and compute. We believe the bitter lesson applies just as much to computer vision, 3D and spatial understanding, in the end to visual intelligence: the effort should go into general architectures that solve all these tasks in a unified way, and into scaling them, rather than into task-specific designs. We test this on monocular geometry, a task that has belonged to specialised architectures for years. Iris-Z is a general image generator that produces appearance and geometry together. A single backbone denoises, reasons about and understands the image and its depth at the same time, and one set of weights generates images with their depth, estimates depth from photographs and paints images onto a given depth. At enough scale it surpasses state-of-the-art specialists on established depth benchmarks while keeping the generative abilities of the backbone it started from without specialized layers. We release two models for the community.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:More text-to-video models are available, but many remain limited to low resolutions. Training them for higher resolutions is costly, while existing training-free methods still struggle with fine detail, scene structure, and efficiency. We propose TRACE, a training-free method that synchronizes low- and high-resolution generation trajectories. During early denoising, TRACE aligns predicted scene content at corresponding noise levels. 93. 【2611.15968】DuoJEPA: Cross-Modal Translation Between Unaligned Self-Supervised Embedding Spaces

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15968

作者：Mahmut S. Gokmen,Mitchell A. Klusty,Evan W. Damron,Caroline N. Leach,Samuel E. Armstrong,V.K. Cody Bumgardner

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：modalities is commonly, commonly equated, equated with aligning, embedding spaces remain, shared space

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Learning from paired modalities is commonly equated with aligning them: two encoders are trained until the embeddings of a pair coincide in a shared space. We study whether cross-modal retrieval and classification can instead be obtained while the two encoders' own embedding spaces remain directly unaligned. DuoJEPA trains one encoder per modality with the self-supervised LeJEPA objective and appends to each encoder a single transformer layer that translates its embedding into the other encoder's space, supervised by a contrastive objective computed entirely within that target space. No loss term compares the two own spaces directly, although the encoders are optimised jointly through the translation objective. In our experiments, the translator's residual connections determine where the cross-modal pressure is absorbed: with a residual translator the two encoders become directly aligned, whereas with a skipless (residual-free) translator a paired-cosine test detects no alignment in the final models, and at most a weak one in the ablations, at comparable cross-modal performance. The learned class-token translation is well approximated by a single scaled rotation of the source space. We evaluate DuoJEPA on two modality pairs that differ in nearly every respect. On chest radiographs and reports from CheXpert Plus, it attains a zero-shot AUC of 86.6 and a cross-modal retrieval score of 78.5, compared with 60.0 and 64.2 for our reimplementation of LeVLJEPA with the same encoders and data. On Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 radar imagery, the frozen encoders fall within the range of published Sentinel foundation models under our reproduction of the Copernicus-Bench linear-probe protocol, the translator retrieves the paired image of the other sensor among 4,000 candidates in 77-79% of cases, and a patch-level extension transfers the within-image similarity structure of the scene.

Abstract:Controllable real-world video generation models can condition on a rendered graybox proxy video, which fixes layout, motion and camera. We introduce VibePrevisBench, a benchmark for 4D scene reconstruction: an agent turns a brief into an executable 4D scene and renders its proxy video.
