Agents thus need better visual understanding of space and camera, explicit checks for challenging requests, and item-by-item verification of renders against the brief.

100. 【2611.15766】Prior-as-Skill: Remember Outside and Read Inside with On-Policy Self-Distillation for Self-Evolving VLM Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15766

作者：Yan Lin,Jingyu Sun,Yifei Qian,Zhengtao Yao,Yuxia Wang,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau,Zhongliang Guo,Stephen McGough

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Self-evolving visual agents, Self-evolving visual, visual agents, order, Self-evolving

备注： under review

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-evolving visual agents keep what they learn as skills: key frames with step notes on a task's steps and their order. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is now a popular way to train such skills into a vision-language model that matches, on its own answers, a teacher that also reads the written order. At deployment, frames often go missing or show an unusual order, as when a person or a website reorders the steps. Existing internalization methods keep any order they store in the frame-reading weights: trained on full frames, the model guesses when they are missing, and trained with frames withheld, it stores the order, so memory can override what it sees. We propose PriorSplit, which fills in missing frames from an order kept outside the model, with no training beyond OPSD. It counts which of two neighboring steps comes first in training episodes, and the model checks if both asked steps are in view: if so, it answers from the frames, and otherwise the counts answer. Extensive experiments on synthetic skills with five models and on step-tagged order questions in new episodes of seen web, cooking and egocentric tasks demonstrate that, with frames missing, PriorSplit outperforms every compared method at OPSD's training cost. With Qwen3-VL-4B on synthetic skills, it also stays close to OPSD on unusual orders, where methods that gain most by storing the order fall behind.

101. 【2611.15764】AIFR: Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery for Diffusion-Based Makeup and Anime Portraits

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15764

作者：Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Yaxin Xiao,Mengyu Sun,Xinwei Liu,Renyang Liu,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu

关键词：online content creation, identity-based content screening, anime avatar generation, virtual makeup editing, content creation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion-based makeup and anime portraits are increasingly popular in online content creation (e.g., virtual makeup editing and anime avatar generation), but their altered appearances can hinder face recognition (FR) in identity-based content screening. Existing methods mainly adapt FR encoders or restore input images. The former require costly gallery re-encoding, while the latter may be incomplete or distort identity-related facial details. To address these limitations, we aim to correct identity features within a frozen FR model to recover its output embeddings in its original space. Appearance changes vary in strength and affect facial regions differently. To this end, we propose Adaptive Identity Feature Recovery (AIFR), a framework that inserts a lightweight recovery module at a single intermediate layer to predict sample-level correction strength and spatially adaptive residual updates. Module training uses fused identity targets to guide embedding recovery in the original FR space and an identity-shift score to regulate correction strength and limit unnecessary changes. At inference, AIFR processes a single portrait in one forward pass without reference images, producing recovered embeddings that can be directly matched with existing gallery templates without re-encoding. With only 1.22M trainable parameters, AIFR improves the average TAR at FAR=$10^{-4}$ from 66.1\% to 90.2\% over the original ArcFace, and further experiments show improvements across unseen makeup styles, stylization strengths, and generators. Beyond recognition, the recovered embeddings can directly condition a pretrained Arc2Face model to generate faces with improved identity preservation, showing compatibility with embedding-conditioned downstream generation.

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027. 22 pages, 10 figures. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/DanBotchan/image-deblending-diffusion)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image deblending is the task of decomposing a composite image back into its source images, which is an ill-posed problem, as a single blended image can be decomposed into multiple plausible source layers. While diffusion models are often used to solve inverse problems, they typically address single-output reconstruction problems. In this work, we formulate deblending as a joint inverse problem, employing diffusion posterior sampling to simultaneously reconstruct two source images, demonstrating that a single diffusion prior is expressive enough to disentangle two full images at once rather than just one. Drawing inspiration from human perception, where semantic understanding helps disentangle visual inconsistencies, we propose a training-free framework that leverages text prompts to constrain this solution space. We address the blind deblending task through a two-stage architecture: text-guided latent diffusion performs semantic separation using prompts as high-level cues, followed by a pixel-space refinement to recover fine details. Stochastic sampling produces multiple plausible decompositions that can be ranked without ground truth. Extensive experiments show that our training-free method achieves state-of-the-art results on unseen domains and extends to spatially-varying alpha maps, while remaining competitive with supervised baselines on standard scalar blending benchmarks.

103. 【2611.15756】How Far Can Frozen Vision-Language Models Go on Event Data? A Training-Free Study on EventBench

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15756

作者：Md Kamrujjaman Mobin,Md Salman Shamil,Md Adnan Arefeen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： "Technical report, EBMV @ ECCV 2026 Challenge. 13 pages, 9 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present a training-free system for the EBMV @ ECCV 2026 challenge "Event Stream Understanding with EventBench" (General Understanding track), which evaluates vision-language models on five event-camera understanding tasks. We use none of the challenge's training data: our goal is to measure how far off-the-shelf, RGB-pretrained models can go on event data through representation and prompting alone. For four of the five categories (gesture recognition, detailed understanding, causal reasoning, and event OCR) we render event bins as red/ON, blue/OFF frames, select a category-specific subset of bins, stitch them into a single grid image, and query the same frozen Qwen3.5 9B vision-language model with a category-specific prompt for all four tasks. The fifth category, action recognition, feeds every bin directly into the organizers' EventGPT-Plus-2B. The system reaches 62.28% overall accuracy across 2,084 test questions (rank 9/27), led by action recognition and causal reasoning (80.27%, 77.22%) and limited most by gesture recognition and event OCR (54.70%, 34.57%). We visualize test samples from every category together with the exact images the model receives, score six alternative representations against the evaluation server, and analyze the full test set post hoc. That analysis shows that our bounding-box crop almost never tightened around content, so event OCR text reached the model at roughly one fifth of its horizontal resolution, and that detailed-understanding predictions are skewed toward the first two answer options.

备注： 18 pages, including supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction, overlapping primitives can provide ambiguous class predictions, making their joint interpretation important for voxel classification. However, current methods using spatially weighted mixtures neglect explicit modeling of cross-primitive dependencies, limiting the readout's ability to utilize category-specific relational evidence. Hence, we introduce CGOcc, a supervised multi-camera framework that explicitly models Cross-Gaussian dependencies for 3D semantic Occupancy prediction. CGOcc integrates appearance and geometric observations to refine Gaussian states and encodes cross-Gaussian dependencies in a diagonal-plus-low-rank representation. To incorporate these dependencies into voxel classification, CGOcc uses a class-conditional projection to extract diagonal and signed cross-Gaussian statistics without constructing a dense dependency matrix. The readout then refines occupied-class probabilities using these statistics while preserving a learned reference's occupied mass and free probability. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate CGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with larger gains at high Gaussian overlap and improved predictions near boundaries. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of covariance-aware prediction and support its synergy with geometric interaction.

105. 【2611.15652】aming Neural Fields with a Frequency-Lipschitz Curriculum for Sparse-View CT Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15652

作者：Songtao Hu,Jingrui Wu,Hong Ding,Fanhao Zhu,Yunhao Liu,Suyang Xi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Sparse-view computed tomography, fewer projection views, reduces radiation dose, acquiring fewer projection, resulting under-determined inverse

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sparse-view computed tomography (SVCT) reduces radiation dose by acquiring fewer projection views, but the resulting under-determined inverse problem requires careful regularization to prevent reconstruction algorithms from fitting streak artifacts and hallucinated structures. Existing implicit neural representation (INR) methods rely on implicit spectral bias or iterative external denoising, yet they provide limited control over when high-frequency capacity is exposed during optimization. To address this limitation, we propose FLIC (Frequency-Lipschitz INR Curriculum), a capacity-controlled self-prior INR framework that makes the network's representational capacity explicit and schedulable. The core of our method is a joint frequency-Lipschitz curriculum that couples random Fourier feature encoding with spectrally normalized MLP decoding under non-uniform Lipschitz budgets, guiding reconstruction along a coarse-to-fine trajectory from stable global anatomy to fine details. The terminal capacity is automatically calibrated from FBP gradient statistics, enabling scan-adaptive regularization without per-scan manual tuning of the terminal Lipschitz budget. We further introduce a prior-guided adaptive ray sampling strategy that concentrates integration samples near high-gradient anatomical boundaries, improving forward-model fidelity. The entire framework operates in a single training stage without iterative denoising, enabling efficient per-image reconstruction. Extensive experiments on four public CT datasets under noise-free and low-dose settings show that our method achieves state-of-the-art unsupervised reconstruction quality with consistent performance across diverse anatomical structures.

106. 【2611.15645】Ultrasound Elastography: Excitation Methods and Low-Cost Alternative

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15645

作者：Corin F. Otesteanu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Ultrasound elastography (or elasticity imaging) is a method that characterizes the tissue mechanical properties with the purpose of aiding the diagnosis of certain pathologies. Many novel clinical applications use the assumption that these tissue properties are changed in the presence of pathological processes. Characterization of tissue mechanical properties is achieved by imaging the response of the tissue to an induced stress or excitation. A plethora of approaches have been used in the scientific community to induce excitation in tissue, which usually require additional tools or devices than are costly. Furthermore these are usually integrated in the imaging pipeline using additional software or hardware for control. An alternative that is simpler, faster to integrate (and low-cost) is proposed in this work, by using readily available smartphones as a source of mechanical excitation. The induced displacements are used to solve the elasticity inverse problem (IP) using a finite element method (FEM) approach. Preliminary elasticity reconstructions of a phantom that mimics tissue are similar to when using a dedicated actuator as means of inducing harmonic displacements, illustrating the feasibility of our low-cost ultrasound elasography approach.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Streaming video understanding aims to continuously process incoming visual observations, making the retention and retrieval of relevant information increasingly important as the video history grows. Although existing benchmarks include memory-related questions, question-answering accuracy alone does not reliably measure memory capabilities. Incorrect predictions may arise from failures in visual understanding rather than forgetting, whereas correct predictions may rely on linguistic priors or recent observations without requiring historical memory. To address these ambiguities, we introduce MemInStream, a benchmark dedicated to evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding. We develop an evidence-controlled validation protocol that associates each question with temporally localized evidence intervals. This helps disentangle memory limitations from understanding failures while reducing memory-independent shortcuts. MemInStream provides a systematic evaluation of memory capabilities through four major categories, covering Episodic Retrieval, State Tracking \ Updating, Multi-Evidence Integration, and Contextual Memory across diverse video scenarios. Overall, \textsc{MemInStream} provides a comprehensive and reliable benchmark for systematically evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Content-style decomposition from a single image is a fundamental challenge in personalized image generation, aiming to separate subject identity from visual style for flexible recomposition. Existing approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain, without explicitly exploiting the distinct frequency characteristics naturally associated with content and style. In this paper, we propose WaveletLoRA, a novel frequency-aware framework for explicit content-style decomposition through wavelet-domain supervision. Specifically, we first apply a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to decompose the reference image into low-frequency and high-frequency subbands. In addition, we establish WaveBench and develop a VLM-based evaluation protocol that explicitly measures both attribute similarity and attribute separation for disentanglement assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveletLoRA consistently outperforms existing methods and produces results that better align with human preference.

109. 【2611.15614】Learning-Based Interpolation of Detector Gaps in Photon-Counting X-ray Imaging

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15614

作者：Johannes B. Thalhammer,Patrick Ilg,Tina Dorosti,Sebastian Peterhansl,Theresa Hiu,Daniel Frey,Zijin Huang,Florian Schaff,Daniela Pfeiffer,Franz Pfeiffer

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Image detectors assembled, Image detectors, assembled from tiled, tiled modules, modules have physical

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image detectors assembled from tiled modules have physical gaps in the field of view that must be filled before downstream processing such as phase retrieval or tomographic reconstruction. Unlike natural-image inpainting, detector-gap interpolation must leave all other pixels unmodified and preserve the intensity statistics of the filled region, since even a small shift in mean intensity can cause ring artifacts after reconstruction. We adapted two generative models, a Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty (WGAN-GP) and a conditional Pix2Pix model, to this task. We implemented a masking strategy that restricts all changes to the gap pixels and loss terms that constrain the mean intensity and the intensity distribution of the filled region. Both models were trained on nanoCT projections with simulated gaps and compared with linear interpolation, Navier--Stokes inpainting, and the deep image-inpainting network MADF, quantitatively on simulated gaps and visually on real detector gaps. WGAN-GP and Pix2Pix achieved the best perceptual similarity (LPIPS) and histogram distance and, apart from linear interpolation, the shortest inference time. Pix2Pix required about five times fewer training GPU-hours than WGAN-GP. Ablation studies showed that masking the network output contributes by far the most to plausible inpainting results. Applied to sinograms, Pix2Pix produced the most natural gap filling but improved the reconstruction only slightly compared with linear interpolation. The source code and the weights of the final Pix2Pix model are available at this https URL.

Abstract:Deploying real-time object detection on power-constrained edge platforms is challenging as processing full-resolution frames is often computationally redundant. FoveaDet provides a scalable, efficient architecture capable of unlocking advanced vision processing on severely constrained mobile platforms.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) impose substantial KV cache demands from both large multimodal contexts and growing reasoning traces. Low-rank KV compression has emerged as a promising paradigm that stores low-dimensional latents of KV states and can be combined with quantization. However, existing low-rank methods often induce growing predictive policy drift from the dense model when compressing reasoning KV states, resulting in reasoning failures. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tabletop games offer a compelling scenario to study the interaction between artificial and human agents, combining social interaction with game state tracking and physical manipulation. To assess the game state, a robot needs to precisely detect closely related game objects, such as different card designs, requiring dedicated training data that is costly to annotate in the real world. Instead, we develop a fully automated synthetic data collection pipeline, using re-projection of known 3D coordinates to generate labels automatically and domain randomization to bridge the reality gap. This approach is used to collect a dataset of 25413 samples to finetune an object detection and a segmentation model, which are combined to assign detected cards to game-relevant zones and infer the game state. When evaluated on a real NICO humanoid robot, our pipeline achieves an F1-score of 0.906 for card-to-zone assignment, thus providing the perceptual foundation for a robot that can autonomously play Chef's Hat against human players.

120. 【2611.15514】Real Pairs Are Not Enough: Physically Anchored Supervision for Volumetric CT Super-Resolution

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15514

作者：Haoran Liu,Zipei Yan,Mingzhe Liu,Jizhou Li,Guibin Zan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：physically acquired pairs, commonly trained, Physically-anchored Implicit Network, synthetic pairs miss, pairs retain inter-scan

备注： 47 pages, 5 figures, including supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Volumetric CT super-resolution (SR) models are commonly trained on synthetic or physically acquired pairs of low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) volumes. However, synthetic pairs miss real acquisition effects, whereas registered physical pairs retain inter-scan noise, artifact, and microstructure differences---causing direct voxelwise regression, and even naive synthetic-real mixing, to oversmooth fine porous structures. We introduce the Physically-anchored Implicit Network (PIN) with Real-Pair Dual-Anchor learning, which decouples physical-pair supervision into two complementary roles. The HR anchor teaches fine structure from registered HR through pair-conditioned spectral replay, while the LR anchor constrains the prediction from acquired LR via affine-calibrated low-pass projection. Together with exact synthetic supervision in one shared volumetric implicit model, PIN learns from both physical scans without forcing the acquired-LR reconstruction to match registered HR voxel by voxel. Inference requires only acquired LR. Across 30 external baseline configurations on real CT, PIN achieves the lowest full-volume LPIPS, reduces average symmetric surface distance by 42.3-49.5% and bone-volume-fraction error by 52.2-53.4% relative to the three strongest baselines for each metric, and lowers the erased reference-pore volume fraction from 37.3-72.9% to 10.4%. Code, pretrained models, and evaluation scripts will be publicly available.

121. 【2611.15491】What Makes a VLA Reactive? A Study of Execution Strategies for Dynamic Manipulation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15491

作者：Zhuoyue Li,Zhendong Han,Pankhuri Vanjani,Wenting Li,Rudolf Lioutikov

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：models predict chunks, pretrained VLA reactive, models predict, objects move, VLA reactive

备注： 13 pages, 7 figures, Efficient Foundation Models for Real-Time Embodied AI, CoRL 2026 Workshop

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions, and when objects move, those chunks go stale before they are executed. We study what makes a pretrained VLA reactive, using \pihalf{} on two dynamic simulation benchmarks and three real-robot tasks. We vary the prediction and execution horizons, compare synchronous and asynchronous inference, test methods that deliver actions sooner or keep successive chunks continuous, and adapt the policy with motion cues and latent-space reinforcement learning. The best prediction horizon is about one second, and the best execution horizon depends on the checkpoint. Short horizons help more with asynchronous execution, and methods that keep the trajectory continuous outperform those that only reduce latency. Adapting the policy repairs targeted failures, but on the conveyor task changing the execution horizon gained as much. Reactivity depends on the execution pipeline as much as on the policy, so both should be evaluated together.

Abstract:Recently developed remote sensing (RS) agents execute real-world tasks with complex workflows through multi-step tool invocation. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper presents FOMO (Fear of Memorizing Objects), an end-to-end trainable transformer-based architecture for zero-shot model-based object detection. Existing approaches typically follow a proposal-and-match pipeline: target-agnostic mask proposals are generated and then matched to template features in a set of loosely coupled stages, making them susceptible to fragmented proposals in clutter and degraded matching under occlusions. FOMO elegantly replaces this paradigm by injecting object template information early in the detection pipeline: Bidirectional attention between learnable latent tokens, multi-view CAD templates, and query image features produces object-aware image features and conditioned queries which are subsequently decoded into bounding-box and confidence predictions. We train FOMO on Clutter6D, a novel large-scale synthetic dataset with over 500k images featuring more than 10k diverse object models rendered in highly cluttered scenes with multiple instances and varying occlusion levels, which addresses the limited object diversity and lack of realistic dense clutter in existing datasets for training zero-shot multi-instance algorithms. In true zero-shot fashion, FOMO achieves 54.2 mAP on BOP-Classic-Core at 0.23 s per image, ranking second overall with 3.8 pp difference to the top method while being more than 7x faster. Throughout comprehensive ablation studies we investigate and verify architectural design choices.

124. 【2611.15457】WaveFlow-UIE: A Single-Pipeline Wavelet-Domain Flow Model for Efficient Underwater Image Restoration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15457

作者：Abdulaziz Alfaraj,Mohammed Al Naser,Hassan Al Nasser,Muzammil Behzad

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：image components differently, loss affect low, high-frequency image components, detail loss affect, image restoration remains

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Underwater image restoration remains challenging because color distortion, scattering, and detail loss affect low- and high-frequency image components differently. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown strong restoration quality, but often rely on multi-stage pipelines and costly iterative refinement. We present WaveFlow-UIE, a single-pipeline wavelet-domain flow model that directly learns to transform degraded underwater wavelet representations into clean ones. Our method applies a discrete wavelet transform, estimates lightweight physics-inspired degradation cues including transmission and ambient light, and conditions a velocity network trained with flow matching in wavelet space. The training objective combines flow-matching, frequency-weighted reconstruction, perceptual, color-consistency, and physics-consistency losses. Across UIEB, LSUI, UFO-120, and EUVP, WaveFlow-UIE achieves a strong quality-efficiency tradeoff, with especially competitive results on perceptual metrics. Relative to a strong wavelet-based diffusion baseline, the proposed model substantially reduces inference cost while maintaining or improving restoration quality on several paired benchmarks. We show that reducing the inference process from five flow steps to a single step preserves nearly all of the restoration quality while providing an additional speedup of about $4\times$ on UIEB at $256\times256$ resolution. This single-step result suggests that a single-pass wavelet-domain model is an effective alternative to heavier diffusion-style refinement.

125. 【2611.15449】Reading the Unreadable: Text-Aware Image Super-Resolution Needs Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15449

作者：Jaeseong Lee,Jinwoo Kim,Jinho Jeong,Seon Joo Kim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：preserving textual content, Text-aware image super-resolution, recover high-resolution images, Text-aware image, aims to recover

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-aware image super-resolution (TAISR) aims to recover high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs while preserving textual content. Existing methods treat this as a visual restoration problem, yet severely degraded text often cannot be read from local visual evidence alone. Humans instead read it by reasoning over image context, logical patterns, and prior knowledge. This raises a fundamental question: Does TAISR need reasoning? To answer this, we introduce two diagnostic tools. The first, ReasonText, is a human-curated benchmark that separates reasoning-required text from locally recoverable text. The second, Ground-Truth Text-Crop Accuracy (GTTCA), is a recognition-based metric that measures text restoration quality without text detection errors. Evaluating twelve recent super-resolution (SR) models, we find a consistent and substantial performance drop on reasoning-required text, revealing a systematic blind spot. Two observations point to a remedy. One is that text-conditioned SR models restore text substantially better when given ground-truth text. The other is that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can read degraded text in low-resolution images through reasoning. We therefore propose Reasoning Transfer via Captioning (RTC), a training-free plug-in that uses an external MLLM to infer the degraded text and feeds the resulting caption to a text-conditioned SR model. RTC consistently improves all six text-conditioned SR models we evaluate, especially on reasoning-required text. Surprisingly, a real-world SR model with RTC can even outperform the TAISR model with RTC, suggesting that reasoning should be a core design axis for future TAISR.

126. 【2611.15448】Semantic Consistency of Vision Tokens: A Vision-Centric Perspective on Multimodal Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15448

作者：Nikola Đukić,Tim Lebailly,Tinne Tuytelaars

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：unique paradigms characterized, vision-only patch representations, high-quality vision-only patch, high-quality patch representations, patch representations

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In computer vision, vision-language and self-supervised encoders are two unique paradigms characterized by global language alignment and high-quality vision-only patch representations, respectively. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often use the former because of language alignment, despite the recent rise in vision-centric tasks resembling traditional dense vision tasks that would likely benefit from the high-quality patch representations, raising the question whether self-supervised models could aid MLLMs in vision-centric tasks. To investigate this, we define semantic (in)consistency of patch tokens from language-aligned encoders and identify two sets of tokens, semantically consistent (SCTs) and semantically inconsistent (SITs). Through systematic probing via the removal of SCTs or SITs from the input of modern MLLMs, we show higher reliance on SCTs than SITs, as removal of the former induces larger performance drops. This demonstrates that MLLMs benefit from semantic consistency of vision tokens. With this in mind, we devise a simple yet effective extension of the MLLM training objective that combines traditional language modeling with semantic guidance via minimization of semantic inconsistency. This strategy, named Semantically-Guided Training (SGT), combines the signals of both loss terms to push the vision encoder towards providing semantically consistent tokens, while preserving language alignment. Extensive evaluation shows that SGT outperforms all baselines and MLLMs with specialized vision encoders on vision-centric benchmarks while keeping strong scores on text-centric benchmarks, thus setting state of the art on average across all tasks. Our results highlight the importance of combining language alignment and semantic consistency through the lens of MLLMs rather than independently from them and pave the way towards the design of better MLLM vision encoders.

Our experiments across synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SteinPnP accurately captures complex multimodal uncertainties while maintaining competitive accuracy.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Scene graphs provide a compact, structured representation of environments by capturing objects and their spatial or semantic relationships in a form suitable for embodied agents. Existing 3D scene graph generation methods often rely on depth sensing at inference, either to associate object instances across views via point cloud overlap or to compute inter-object relations from geometric cues, limiting deployment to platforms with depth sensors. We propose IncR-3DSGG, which builds 3D scene graphs incrementally from posed\footnote{RGB images with camera extrinsics estimated by an off-the-shelf visual SLAM system.} RGB images, eliminating the use of depth sensors at inference. This shows that relations and metrically grounded 3D object representations can be predicted from posed RGB images alone.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15420

作者：Anselm Krainovic,Reinhard Heckel

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：degraded measurements requires, measurements requires priors, Reconstructing video, degraded measurements, measurements requires

备注： NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reconstructing video from degraded measurements requires priors that capture both frame appearance and temporal dynamics. Many recent approaches combine pretrained image diffusion priors with motion guidance, sidestepping the costs of training large video models. Temporal consistency is often enforced via photometric losses or warping-based constraints, which implicitly assume that motion explains frame-to-frame or latent-to-latent changes up to small deviations, an assumption that fails under occlusions, lighting changes, and non-rigid deformation. In this paper, we propose to learn the coupling between motion and frame appearance as a generative prior. We learn the distribution of motion-induced appearance residuals with a conditional diffusion model and use it to regularize reconstruction in video inverse problems. Our motion-appearance prior acts as a plug-in regularizer for diffusion-based video reconstruction, preserving the benefits of image diffusion models while remaining compatible with existing appearance regularization techniques. Experiments on dynamic video inverse problems show that reconstruction with a learnt motion-appearance coupling prior outperforms photometric-consistency and noise-warping baselines, and that the approach also transfers to cardiac MRI reconstruction.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language-action (VLA) models bring multimodal understanding and world knowledge to end-to-end autonomous driving. To further improve the quality of predicted trajectories, existing studies introduce candidate refinement, residual denoising, and token correction. However, these methods typically focus on unified refinement of the entire trajectory, paying limited attention to the distinct physical quantities and their interactions. We further investigate physical specialization and coordination in trajectory refinement, centering on position and heading for simpler and more intuitive correction modeling. To this end, we propose CARE-Drive (Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories), which decomposes trajectory refinement into waypoint residual correction and position-conditioned heading generation. This design simplifies refinement through clear physical specialization while preserving conditional coupling during prediction and optimization. On NAVSIM v1, CARE-Drive achieves 88.8 PDMS, improving by 2.4 points over the unrefined base trajectories generated by the same backbone. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of specialized experts and staged training.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language-guided place recognition requires recovering geographic identity across visual observations beyond the image from which a description originates. However, aggregate retrieval scores often entangle observation transfer, gallery competition, and linguistic evidence, obscuring the underlying recognition capability. Each description retains its source-image provenance, while retrieval operates on disjoint target observations. Text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) retrieval share a unified place-identity relevance criterion supporting multiple valid matches. Two geographic protocols evaluate unseen places within represented cities (P0) and generalization to held-out cities (P1). Paired observation comparisons, gallery-controlled evaluations, fixed-query scaling, and blinded annotation auditing systematically characterize retrieval performance. Across three training seeds, place-supervised SigLIP2 T2I Recall@1 decreases from 19.69% on source images to 12.31% on independent targets under P0. For fixed P1 queries, expanding the gallery from 500 to 8,425 places reduces Recall@1 from 14.67% to 2.87%. A 240-description audit further reveals discrepancies between automated acceptance and factual evidence. These findings establish AcrossPlace as a reproducible evaluation standard for assessing transferable place identity across visual observations and geographic environments.

137. 【2611.15311】Arbor: When Labels Are Not Coarsened at Random

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15311

作者：Faisal Mohammad,Mohamed Younis

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Merging datasets labelled, Merging datasets, coarse label, coarse, Coarse labels

备注： 26 pages, 7 figures, 25 tables. Appendices included

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Merging datasets labelled at different levels of a taxonomy leaves some images with only a coarse label, and some classes with no fine-grained label at all. Standard training scores a coarse label by the total probability of the classes under it. Coarse labels name superclasses, and the standard objective is exact only when every class within a superclass is equally likely to receive a coarse label. We call a class novel if it is never labelled finely, and known otherwise. A novel class violates the assumption, and the classifier assigns its images to a known sibling. We propose Arbor, which models how labels become coarse. In each superclass with a novel class, Arbor adds a virtual novel class, and a logistic gate divides the superclass's probability between it and the known classes. The gate is trained with the likelihood of the observed labels under this model, the coarsening likelihood. We prove that at its population optimum the gate equals the Elkan-Noto correction of positive-unlabelled learning, and show that training the correction into the classifier improves on applying it after training. Across Cars, CUB and Aircraft with 90% coarse labels, Arbor correctly assigns 76-85% of novel-class images to their superclass, more than hierarchical classifiers, hierarchical novelty detection and the Elkan-Noto correction. Its top-1 accuracy over the leaf classes on known-class images is within 1.6 points of the best baseline, although its final predictions return the superclass more often. On the more challenging iNaturalist-2019 benchmark, Arbor improves novel-class accuracy from 46% (uniform target, the strongest baseline) to 68%.

Abstract:We study how to repurpose video diffusion models (VDMs), pretrained on millions of videos, for dense 3D point tracking, where training data is scarce. We thus systematically assess the effectiveness of two such video representations. The first, Residual 3D Tracking Video (RTV), encodes per-pixel 3D offsets relative to a query frame and allows recovery of 3D trajectories directly from the generated video. The second, Normalized Coordinate Map Video (NCMV), labels each pixel with a canonical coordinate invariant through time. This is closer to a natural video and easier for the VDM to learn, but requires additional post-processing with an off-the-shelf 2D tracker to recover 3D tracks. RTV tracks more accurately, while NCMV shows that a VDM can generate dense correspondence as a video. We instantiate both representations with LoRA adaptation of a frozen Wan backbone, conditioned on point maps from an off-the-shelf 3D reconstructor as a geometric scaffold. Trained with a single dynamic 3D tracking dataset, Track4D achieves competitive zero-shot tracking on four real and synthetic benchmarks. A regression readout that bypasses the video VAE and uses additional training data brings further improvement, demonstrating the potential of this direction for dense 3D tracking. We hope our analysis will inspire future work that leverages VDM priors for dense 3D tracking and 4D reconstruction. Project page: this https URL

141. 【2611.15260】Driving World Models Move the Camera, Not the Scene

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15260

作者：Yuqiao Feng,Xiaoyu Ao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Plan-conditioned driving world, control metrics check, compare candidate plans, Plan-conditioned driving, driving world models

备注： 23 pages. Project page: [this https URL](https://joefeng0571.github.io/retime-compose/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Plan-conditioned driving world models are used to compare candidate plans, so their forecast should change with the plan as the real scene would, yet widely used control metrics check only whether the camera follows the command. Recorded drives already contain real outcomes of plans that were never driven: retiming a log gives pose-matched images and LiDAR for other speeds, and a stereo rig records a laterally shifted view. Against these references, released world models move their camera but barely move the scene: their scene response, the share of the real change in distance that the forecast reproduces, reaches at most about a third of real video's, and the trajectory score cannot tell an executed speed command from an ignored one. Interventions inside the generator trace the failure to the plan's channel. The forecast geometry follows the context frames; once they carry the planned geometry, plan tokens add little. ReTime-Compose therefore delivers the plan as geometry, without training. It transports the frames of one logged-speed rollout to the planned cameras, and the frozen model completes only what no frame covers. Frozen generators then follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly as the real scene does on KITTI, nuScenes and Argoverse 2, and respond more than re-sampling to slow-down plans in three generators and to speed-up plans in two.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15216

作者：Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Rang Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:\textbf{Personalized Image Segmentation (PIS)} aims to segment the specific object in a target image that matches a user-provided reference. Unlike category-based segmentation or few-shot segmentation, PIS must discriminate between multiple visually similar instances, a requirement central to deployed applications such as augmented reality, personalized content editing, robotics manipulation, inventory tracking, and fine-grained visual retrieval. We show that the strong numbers reported by existing PIS methods are largely an artifact of single-instance benchmarks: when evaluated on realistic scenes with several same-category look-alikes, prior point-prompt methods collapse, because the correct instance is frequently never surfaced as a prompt. We trace this to a recall bottleneck rather than a mask-quality one, and address it with a training-free, modular system. The pipeline first generates high-recall object proposals using an off-the-shelf class-agnostic detector, then matches each proposal to the reference using foundation-model features (e.g., BLIP-2), and finally refines the selected box with SAM. For robust matching we propose \textbf{top-$K$ Sinkhorn}, a patch-level distance that scores each candidate only on its most confidently matched patches; trimming the Sinkhorn dual potentials recovers the robustness of partial optimal transport at more than $100\times$ lower cost, keeping it inside the per-proposal candidate loop. To evaluate this regime, we introduce \textbf{\datasetname}, a realistic multi-instance benchmark. Across \datasetname, PerSeg, PerMIS, and DAVIS, our system surpasses PerSAM, $\mathrm{P}^2\mathrm{SAM}$, and PDM by up to $\sim\!48$ mIoU while matching their runtime and using fewer parameters, establishing a strong, deployable training-free baseline for real-world personalized segmentation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Window-based next-act evaluation often aims to measure generalisation across dialogues, yet overlapping windows and similarity retrieval can expose labelled examples from the test dialogue. We induce this exposure under controlled designs on SGD, STAR and AMI, whose official splits are clean. Exposure frequency depends on the retrieval pipeline. We evaluate two diagnostic comparisons without model retraining and find that excluding the test dialogue does not detectably reduce accuracy on SGD. All exposure is induced; no published system was re-evaluated.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment a target class in images from a previously unseen domain using only a few annotated support examples. Training-free CD-FSS methods, which typically leverage vision foundation models such as DINOv3, achieve competitive performance without parameter updates. However, the optimal DINOv3 layer for mask prediction varies across support--query episodes. Although several layer-selection criteria have been proposed, they do not explicitly evaluate whether the predicted query foreground and background labels agree with the corresponding support labels. To address this limitation, we propose a novel layer-selection criterion, Optimal-Transport-Based Backbone Layer Selection (OT-BLS). Our key insight is that an effective layer-selection criterion can be derived from the optimal-transport correspondence between query and support patch-token features. Based on this insight, we first estimate the transport plan using a well-established optimal transport algorithm and then compute a layer-selection score by aggregating the transported mass according to the foreground--background partition defined by each candidate query mask. The resulting score reflects how well the predicted foreground and background of the query correspond to those of the support. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of OT-BLS. It outperforms the conventional last-layer baseline in 12 of 16 dataset/shot settings. Furthermore, OT-BLS improves the average mIoU from 55.72 to 57.62 and achieves a 2.99-point improvement over the best-performing existing layer-selection criterion while running 3.7--14.6$\times$ faster.

148. 【2611.15168】MAST: Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering for Visual Grounding in Multimodal LLMs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15168

作者：Zhitao Chen,Zhenxin Cai,Jingyuan Li,Jin Sha

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：large language models, multimodal large language, Existing activation steering, Existing activation, language models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing activation steering methods for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) use the global activation mean as the steering vector, which tends to cause cross-task interference and unstable performance. We trace this failure to the multi-centered geometry of task activations. The global mean lands in the empty region between clusters and is faithful to no single task. Moreover, the steering signal in each task-pure local cluster is low-rank, and different query tasks benefit from emphasizing different families of local-basis directions under the shared routing protocol. To address this, we propose MAST (Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering), a training-free, input-adaptive activation steering framework. MAST groups samples by task, performs multi-center clustering within each group, and selects one representative medoid per cluster. At inference time, each query is routed to multiple representative medoids. For each matched cluster, a low-rank local steering direction is extracted and aggregated across clusters to produce the final steering vector. Building on MAST, MAST-PR (Probe-Reweighted) uses a lightweight task-aware probe to reweight task-specialized local-direction families, yielding a further average improvement of +8.09 points over MAST.

149. 【2611.15165】Bridging Update Coordination and Flatness in Federated Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15165

作者：Chongwei He,Shichen Huang,Jiayun Li,Deyuan Lin,Wangze Jiang,Shengjia Li,Xu Wang,Xiaofan Li,Zhi-Ri Tang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：sharing raw samples, combines complementary knowledge, raw samples, Federated learning combines, limitations without sharing

备注： 16 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Federated learning combines complementary knowledge across clients to alleviate local data limitations without sharing raw samples. However, data heterogeneity can cause conflicting updates and hinder generalization. Update coordination reduces interference among client updates, while flatness optimization reduces sensitivity to parameter perturbations to improve generalization. Existing joint designs largely target specific algorithms, limiting flexible combinations of these approaches. To address this problem, we propose a unified federated learning framework that bridges update coordination and flatness optimization. Clients separately accumulate descent signals and flatness corrections along the same local trajectory. The server coordinates both groups with independent states before fusing their outputs into a global update. Explicit conversion between trajectory signals and model updates enables different coordination strategies to work with compatible local or globally guided flatness mechanisms without changing the inference architecture. Experiments show that the evaluated combinations generally improve average and worst-client accuracy over update coordination alone, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in combining these complementary optimization mechanisms.

CineMME provides a compact diagnostic testbed for evaluating how multimodal video models connect dialogue, speakers, visual evidence, and narrative meaning.

151. 【2611.15153】MatrixSplat: Matrix-Parallel and Feature-Scalable 3D Gaussian Splatting Rendering on GPUs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15153

作者：Yangming Zhang,Siyi Wu,Jian Wang,Bingzhe Li,Wei Niu,Sheng Di,Ming Li,Miao Yin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Tensor Cores, mainstream representation, representation for real-time, real-time novel view, view synthesis

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a mainstream representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction. However, existing 3DGS rendering pipelines are poorly aligned with modern GPU architectures: depth-sorted alpha compositing introduces strong per-pixel sequential dependencies, limiting parallelism and resulting in scalar FMA-dominated execution that underutilizes high-throughput matrix units such as Tensor Cores. This mismatch becomes more severe for multimodal and semantically enriched 3DGS, where high-dimensional features must be accumulated from millions of Gaussians, leading to rapidly increasing computation and memory costs. We propose MatrixSplat, a matrix-parallel rendering framework for feature-scalable 3DGS on modern GPUs. We first identify the sequential dependency of alpha compositing as a key obstacle to efficient high-dimensional feature rendering, and adopt an order-independent weighted accumulation formulation that transforms feature rendering into matrix-oriented computation. Our framework features three key designs: (i) a tile-based splatting pipeline that maps feature accumulation to warp-level matrix-multiply-accumulate execution on Tensor Cores; (ii) a warp-granularity Gaussian packing strategy that mitigates visibility irregularity and improves feature reuse; and (iii) a lazy channel-batched feature-fetching pipeline that reduces shared-memory pressure, maintains high warp occupancy, and overlaps memory access with matrix execution for high-dimensional features. On Mip-NeRF 360, LERF, and 3D-OVS, MatrixSplat achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over 3DGS and 2$\times$ over TC-GS while maintaining rendering quality and semantic accuracy, and further reduces render-kernel time by up to 36.7$\times$ for high-dimensional features. Integrated into existing fast renderers, it provides an additional 1.69-2.84$\times$ speedup.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15149

作者：Qinghui Gong,Jinshan Lai,Xunlei Chen,Hua Meng,Zhengchun Zhou

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recent advances, flow-matching Transformers, Transformers have greatly, risks producing illegal, greatly improved

备注： 23 pages,15 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in flow-matching Transformers have greatly improved Text-to-Image (T2I) and Text-to-Video (T2V) generation, but unconstrained generation risks producing illegal or unethical content. Safety alignment must therefore suppress unsafe content while preserving legitimate generation. A concept's safety depends on context: a knife used for cooking is safe, whereas its use in violence is harmful. Yet most concept erasure methods indiscriminately suppress static, isolated representations, overlooking evolving semantic interactions. Consequently, safe scenes containing potentially sensitive terms may be unintentionally suppressed, compromising generation quality. To address this, we propose Dynamic Graph Semantic Recomposition (DGSR), which dynamically constructs and updates a semantic interaction graph from evolving text visual states during sampling. Context aware graph reasoning captures semantic dependencies, while paired safe/unsafe supervision distinguishes harmful from legitimate interactions, enabling precise identification of and selective intervention on high risk semantic relationships while preserving benign semantics. DGSR freezes the pretrained backbone and supports both image and video generation. Experiments show that DGSR achieves the lowest Attack Success Rate (ASR) in 10 of 12 T2I evaluations and reduces the average T2V unsafe frame rate by 41.2\% relative to the strongest baseline, while maintaining high generation fidelity and temporal consistency.

153. 【2611.15146】Reframing Video Inverse Tone Mapping as Blind Posterior Inference via Latent Luminance Supervision

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15146

作者：Hanwool Sul,Chang-Hoon Jeong,Kisung Shin,Bomseok Park,Donghoon Cha,Hansl von Kwon,DoHyung Ku,Byoung-Tak Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：standard dynamic range, archival footage, dynamic range, HDR, standard dynamic

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:High-dynamic-range (HDR) displays are now common, but most video content, such as archival footage, broadcast material, and the output of generative video models, exists only in standard dynamic range (SDR). The need is sharpest for generated video, which has no HDR master at all and is graded alongside live-action and visual-effects material finished in HDR. Converting SDR to HDR means undoing a tone-mapping operation that was never recorded, and clipping destroys the radiance wherever it acts, so HDR recovery is a blind inverse problem. Methods built on generative video priors fill clipped regions with plausible structure, but their reconstructions are systematically biased: in every public method we test, saturated content leans too bright or too dark, and the direction of the lean moves with the tone operator behind the input. We call this failure the luminance error, measure both its magnitude and its sign, and make it the focus of this work. Reframing ITM as posterior inference under an unknown operator locates the failure in two places and yields two corrections. A fixed affine functional of the video-diffusion latent reads log-luminance without decoding; on that coordinate an asymmetric loss selects an expectile of the posterior, applied only where clipping leaves luminance unconstrained. We also synthesize training SDR through an explicit family of tone operators, so the model marginalizes over what the observation cannot show. Trained this way, our luminance error stays flatter across operators and smaller on average than any public method, and the full system achieves the best perceptual quality on all three benchmarks.

155. 【2611.15131】Duo: A Reciprocal World Model for Full-Duplex Interaction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15131

作者：Hengyuan Cao,Biao Gong,Guoshan Lu,Qiyuan Zhang,Lin Liu,Yiding Tian,Yilei Jiang,Yuxin Ma,Chengzhang Wu,Kun Chen,Yiqian Xie,Ting Liu,Xin Zheng,Xijun Gu,Tianren Ma,Jiancheng Pan,Chi Zhang,Xuzhe Zheng,Siyi Li,Zhenzhong Lan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Sound (cs.SD)

关键词：Reciprocal World Model, Reciprocal World, World Model, RWM treats intelligence, World

备注： [this https URL](https://duo-v1.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this report, we introduce the Reciprocal World Model (RWM) and present Duo v1, a full-duplex world model that continuously perceives audiovisual inputs while responding to users in real time. The RWM treats intelligence and physical reality as equally fundamental to what constitutes a world. Existing world models largely focus on simulating the latter, leaving reasoning and decision-making as external controls rather than intrinsic parts of the world itself. In RWM, we identify two equally fundamental capabilities: Intent, which provides the cognition underlying world interactions, and Realization, which generates coherent audio-visual behavior. To realize this formulation, Duo v1 introduces three key technical designs: (1) Leveraging autoregressive modeling for continuous interaction reasoning and participation decisions, rather than restricting it to instruction understanding for diffusion models. (2) Constructing chunk-specific conditions and synchronize tokens across Intent and Realization on a unified timeline. Module-specific attention masks enforce causal dependencies both within and across chunks, preventing access to future inputs. (3) Mitigating distribution shift in both modules through dual rollout distillation, training on autoregressively generated Intent tokens and synthesized audiovisual histories from Realization. Both modules continuously perceive real-time audio, video, and text, enabling full-duplex interaction with spontaneous conversations and interruptions. Beyond conversation, users can share real objects, reshape virtual scenes, guide assistants, or play audiovisual games without mouse or keyboard controls. The release of Duo v1 makes our first step, with the next version coming soon. We look forward to broader exploration of this emerging field.

159. 【2611.15082】Prediction or Persistence? What Future-Prediction Scores Miss About World Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15082

作者：Animesh Maheshwari

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Pretrained video world, robot learning largely, Pretrained video, video world models, compared and chosen

备注： Accepted at the CoRL 2026 Workshop "Do Robots Need World Models?"

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pretrained video world models are compared and chosen as components for robot learning largely by future-prediction scores. On its own, such a score cannot be read as evidence of prediction, because it mixes encoder quality, the near future's resemblance to the recent past, and the predictor's own contribution. We separate the three with a matched control. On 27,000 future-retrieval trials from household egocentric video, a model must pick the true continuation of a clip among eleven candidates from the same video (chance 0.091), ranked zero-shot by its own training criterion. Each model is paired with a persistence control, an identical copy whose predictor is never invoked, so the paired difference isolates the predictor. Score and contribution dissociate within the native-video V-JEPA family alone: the predictor adds +0.291 top-1 for V-JEPA 2.1 but +0.03 to +0.04 for V-JEPA 2 on the identical trials. Across five models the contribution ranges from +0.291 to negative, and it is negative only for the two action-conditioned models, which we evaluate outside their training regime. Persistence alone recovers the encoder ordering expected from pretraining, a sign that the probe tracks real signal. We suggest reporting each future-prediction score together with its persistence baseline. The gap between them, not the score, is what makes the number interpretable: a necessary check costing one forward pass. Establishing downstream usefulness is a separate matter, for a planning or control evaluation.

160. 【2611.15079】SAVE: Less is More in Agentic Video Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15079

作者：Tymoteusz Wilk,Sergey Prokudin,Xi Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：frameworks iteratively explore, Agentic, video, iteratively explore, observe based

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic video-reasoning frameworks iteratively explore a video, choosing what to observe based on the query and evidence gathered so far. Existing methods are typically evaluated with different backbones, preprocessing pipelines, and computational budgets, making it difficult to determine which components actually drive performance, how much they cost, and whether agentic inference is preferable to native video reasoning in the first place. We conduct a controlled evaluation of a diverse set of recent agentic video-reasoning systems across two strong contemporary backbones, with explicit token and monetary-cost accounting. Across Gemini 3 Flash and Gemma 4, most existing agentic frameworks fail to consistently improve over native video inference and often increase computational cost. We investigate two primary questions: (1) How much agentic framework complexity is necessary for effective video reasoning? (2) When, if at all, is agentic video reasoning preferable to just using the backbone directly? To answer the first question, we introduce a deliberately minimal Active Exploration Baseline and show that it remains competitive with substantially more elaborate agents. We then propose targeted extensions to this baseline, yielding SAVE (Simple Agentic Video Explorer). SAVE outperforms all evaluated prior agentic methods in five of six backbone-benchmark settings. Turning to the second question, our results indicate that the advantage of agentic exploration grows when dense processing would otherwise represent answer-relevant events too sparsely, particularly for long videos and temporally concentrated evidence. Overall, our results suggest that agentic video reasoning should favor simple frameworks over increasingly elaborate pipelines and focus primarily on long-form video, where selective evidence acquisition offers the clearest advantage over native inference.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15047

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Frozen vision-language models often match concepts mentioned in a caption without respecting their negation: a query "a kitchen with no toaster" favors an image containing a toaster. Yet these models are substantially stronger at matching affirmative descriptions. We exploit this asymmetry with Negation-Aware Evidence Scoring (NAES), which reformulates negated descriptions into affirmative queries and combines their evidence by caption structure. NAES also improves negated-description retrieval on COCO and MSR-VTT, with gains extending to SigLIP, SigLIP 2, and larger CLIP encoders. The strongest multiple-choice results use competing descriptions to center and align evidence; retrieval improvements persist without this conditioning. These findings show that explicitly separating affirmative matching from denied-object evidence can substantially improve negation-aware ranking.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15044

169. 【2611.14970】Poison-then-Hide: Finetuning-Activated Backdoor Attack on Pretrained Vision Encoders

作者：Qixuan Jin,Abinitha Gourabathina,Vinith Menon Suriyakumar,Walter Gerych,Marzyeh Ghassemi

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Backdoor attacks threaten the integrity of machine learning models by allowing attackers to control model behavior through triggers. Models can be compromised during finetuning by backdoor attacks through either poisoned data or adversarial training objectives. Because existing finetuning-activated attacks assume limited domain shift or frozen encoder layers, they often fail under full-model finetuning. We target a stealthy finetuning-activated attack where a dormant backdoor is implanted in a pretrained encoder, and later activated by finetuning on clean downstream data. We propose Poison-then-Hide, a novel attack that remains effective when the entire model is finetuned in a domain transfer. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:The geometric understanding of a scene is a crucial component for safe and efficient robotic operations in novel and unstructured environments. The recent emergence of foundation models for Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) has provided an opportunity to enrich robotic perception capabilities with dense long-range depth predictions, replacing expensive onboard hardware (e.g., LiDARs and stereo cameras). However, these offline models are primarily trained on affine-invariant depth representations, and their generalization to novel scenes remains unclear. In this work we present EM-ViT, a monocular depth estimation model that learns to adapt online relative depth inputs from a foundation model prior to the metric scale with observations collected during robotic operations. Motivated by the stereo camera parameterization, our method decomposes depth prediction via dense disparity refinement to recover the scene's relative structure and an effective baseline scalar prediction to determine the missing metric scale. The learning process leverages sparse LiDAR points and, optionally, the robot's Ego-Motion (EM) to rapidly train a lightweight Vision Transformer (ViT), while an internal gradient matching loss preserves the fine-grained semantic structure of the input disparity in real-time. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on publicly available sequences, such as KITTI, and show that our online learning pipeline can adapt to novel scenes faster and with higher accuracy than existing MDE foundation models. The code and supplementary materials will be publicly available at this https URL.

172. 【2611.14942】Emergent Vision-Shaped Thinking in Text-Only Reasoning through Multimodal Training

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14942

作者：Qi Zhang,Bohan Wang,Qianru Sun,Hanwang Zhang

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multimodal training shape, language model, multimodal training, multimodal, training shape

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Does multimodal training shape how a language model thinks, even when the input is text only? We test this with targeted causal ablations across 10 multimodal configurations from 6 model families, with matched text-only backbones and cross-family transfer controls. We contrast spatial-imagery tasks that invite mental scene construction with symbol-manipulation tasks solvable by symbolic rules alone. We identify a vision-language axis in hidden-state space and ablate its projection at inference. In Chameleon-7B, this ablation drops accuracy on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) navigate by 27.6 pp while leaving boolean expressions intact (+5.6 pp), a 33.2 pp double dissociation. The effect is direction-specific and layer-localized, extends to mental-simulation tasks such as mental rotation, paper folding, and perspective taking, and is not explained by task difficulty, reasoning depth, or sequential update load. Together, these results reveal emergent vision-shaped thinking (EVT): text-only reasoning that causally recruits representations introduced or strengthened by vision-language training, without assuming an image-like internal format. The measured effect is spatially concentrated at 7-8B and broadens to non-spatial visual attribute binding in the tested 32B configuration. In dual-path unified models (BAGEL, Janus-Pro), the causal handle localizes to the generation pathway as a compact one-dimensional carrier. The understanding pathway also encodes spatial information in a higher-rank, probe-decodable form, but is not the causal handle. To separate decodability from causal use, we analyze concentration, alignment, and semantic location. These diagnostics explain why behavior, probing, and intervention can diverge. Multimodal training thus reshapes the geometry of text-only reasoning, not just how visual input is processed.

173. 【2611.14941】Reality Perception Gap: Are AI-Manipulated Video Detectors Ready for the Real World?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14941

作者：Xiangrui Liu,Xinmiao Luan,Xinyuan Wang,Zhiyuan Fang,Chi-Yao Huang,Douglas C. Montgomery,Yanjie Fu,Felix Juefei-Xu,YeZhou Yang

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI-manipulated video detectors are typically evaluated on clean, curated datasets. In deployment, however, videos have usually passed through repost compression on social-media platforms and sometimes were captured by imperfect hardware. To measure detection under these conditions, we introduce Reality Perception Gap (RPG), a benchmark of 10,000 videos from frontier generators and partially synthetic edits, processed with cross-platform repost compression and paired with real footage that carries camera-side optical artifacts. RPG also includes socially fragile content, such as crime and wartime footage, where fabricated videos can cause severe social harm. Across 18 detectors, none catches more than 15% of fakes at a 1% false-positive rate. A positive control and a re-encoding sweep separate two sources of failure: generator and content shift degrades detectors before any compression, and repost compression erases the low-level traces that the artifact-based detectors relies on. Existing detectors therefore struggle when deployment conditions strip away the generation artifacts they rely on. These complementary failures suggest that robust detection requires training under deployment conditions and combining low-level, temporal, and semantic evidence, where passive detection may have reached its limits.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14931

作者：Kunze Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Kang Li,Qixing Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：provide relevant visual, visually similar objects, relevant visual evidence, tokens provide relevant, query among visually

备注： 11 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables. The first two authors contributed equally

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reasoning segmentation requires identifying the target referred to by a query among visually similar objects, yet trajectory-level rewards cannot directly indicate which tokens provide relevant visual evidence. We propose Hint-Informed Self-Distillation (HISD), which uses target-disambiguation evidence for token-level credit assignment. An external model generates Segment Hints offline from training images, queries, and ground-truth target annotations, organizing target identity, discriminative attributes, confusable objects, and anchoring paths. A hint-conditioned exponential moving average teacher scores the student's own trajectories, and HISD computes the teacher-to-student evidence ratio over a token support set to adjust only the magnitudes of positive group-relative advantages, leaving nonpositive advantages unchanged. This design encourages concise reasoning while retaining the information needed for target disambiguation, and the student requires neither hints nor a teacher at inference. Under a unified training protocol, HISD improves ReasonSeg test gIoU and cIoU over our reproduction of DPAD by 3.00 and 3.55 percentage points, respectively. Across the five evaluated splits of ReasonSeg and the RefCOCO family, HISD achieves higher segmentation metrics than this baseline and reduces the average token count of valid reasoning spans by 80.17 to 91.83 percent.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14907

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Assessing human creativity from drawings provides a scalable way to evaluate human creative ability. However, existing approaches either rely on costly expert annotation or treat creativity as a single regression target, overlooking its underlying structure. Psychological theories have long emphasized that creativity emerges from balancing novelty and appropriateness. Motivated by this principle, we recast creativity as structured semantic deviation between paired drawings, where novelty measures the extent of departure from the original base sketch, and appropriateness determines whether such departures remain meaningful. Beyond performance gains, our framework reveals an interpretable radial organization of creative variation, offering a principled geometric foundation for automatic creativity assessment.

177. 【2611.14895】HeadIO: Body-Frame Heading Decomposition for Neural Inertial Odometry on Head-Mounted Devices

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14895

作者：Taeyoung Yeon,Yiquan Li,Chenfeng Gao,Vasco Xu,Karan Ahuja

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Inertial Measurement Units, Measurement Units, Inertial Measurement, enables camera-free head, camera-free head tracking

备注： Submitted to ICRA 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Inertial odometry (IO) using only Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) enables camera-free head tracking in head-mounted devices such as VR headsets, AR glasses, and earbuds. Prior IO methods operate in a gravity-aligned world frame, which is not yaw-equivariant. Yaw augmentation improves robustness but does not guarantee equivariance; learned equivariant layers achieve it, but EqNIO's full pipeline costs nearly 3$\times$ the FLOPs of ours. While raw body-frame IMU inputs are inherently yaw-invariant, head tilt redistributes both gravity and motion signals across device axes, making the representation tilt-variant. We propose HeadIO, which combines a body-frame heading decomposition with a bidirectional Mamba backbone for head-mounted inertial odometry. Our decomposition achieves tilt invariance by separating gravity-aligned and horizontal components using only the tilt component of device orientation. We evaluate on four head-mounted datasets and find that HeadIO improves RTE-5s by 8.1% (0.260 m $\to$ 0.239 m) over world-frame methods with learned equivariant layers on Nymeria, the largest benchmark dataset, at nearly 3$\times$ lower FLOPs and 8$\times$ fewer parameters. HeadIO achieves state-of-the-art performance on our primary metric, RTE-5s, on all Aria-glasses datasets, which natively provide body-frame semantics for our decomposition method. We further evaluate the IMU-only deployment scenario on Nymeria, where HeadIO remains state-of-the-art. We show that our method relies mainly on tilt accuracy, which can be obtained from the gravity signal; for yaw, a single-coefficient gyroscope correction removes 11.6% of the yaw error and improves RTE-5s by 2.5%, and sparse external yaw corrections (for example, one every 30 s) can improve RTE-5s by 12.0%. Our decomposition is lightweight, composes with any backbone, and is suitable for real-time deployment on head-mounted devices.

179. 【2611.14856】Honest Fusion Evaluation for Cross-Domain Deepfake Detection: Leakage Audits and Tail Robustness

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14856

作者：Luke Collins,Chang-Tsun Li,Duc Thanh Nguyen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Cross-domain video deepfake, deepfake detection increasingly, video deepfake detection, per-video router picking, frozen foundation-model experts

备注： Accepted at WACV 2027. 19 pages including supplementary material; 6 figures, 10 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/lukegcollins/WACV27-HFEFCDD)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-domain video deepfake detection increasingly uses frozen foundation-model experts, combined by calibrated score averaging, a per-video router picking one expert, or a learned head over concatenated features. Expressive routing and feature fusion are often reported to beat plain averaging. Across a 22-dataset frozen-expert testbed, we find those gains are largely evaluation artifacts. A corner theorem locates the gap routing aims to close: because AUC depends only on score ordering, the best any score-space router can do is an oracle reading the test label to pick each video's expert, a ceiling no realisable, label-blind router reaches. The theorem does not order real routers against a calibrated global average; the case against routing is empirical, and no router we test beats that average. Feature fusion reads raw features, falls outside the theorem, and is audited empirically. Reported gains trace to leakage: holding out one dataset leaks siblings sharing identities and reals (over-crediting routing), while holding out one manipulation keeps that dataset's reals in training (over-crediting feature fusion). A $2\times 2$ over fold and evaluation granularity pins the $+2.6$pp feature-fusion "win" to the fold alone: 12/12 significant under leaky folds vs. 0/12 under honest family-held-out folds, where both gains vanish. Fusion still pays off in the tail of hard videos and families: as experts are added, videos every expert misclassifies drop from 15.2% to 2.3%, and global calibrated fusion (InvB) beats the best single expert on worst-family AUC, selective risk (AURC), and low-false-positive recall, stably across protocols. Practitioners should calibrate and combine globally, hold out whole dataset families, and measure tail performance. For score-space routing under ranking metrics, honest evaluation and expert diversity matter more than combiner complexity.

181. 【2611.14838】Sophon: A Procedural Diagnostic for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14838

作者：J. Zach Gazak,Ryan Swindle,Justin Fletcher

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：simple geometric questions, Open-weight Vision-Language Models, BLINK Spatial, score 85-94, collapse to chance

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) score 85-94% on spatial benchmarks (BLINK Spatial, What's Up CLEVR) yet collapse to chance on simple geometric questions when object, scene, and texture priors are stripped away. We introduce and open-source Sophon, a procedurally generated diagnostic of paired colored point-cloud stimuli across three coordinate systems (1D linear, 2D Cartesian, 2D polar) and four question types (binary, categorical, comparative, quantitative). Across four open architectures (LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-OneVision, Qwen2.5-VL, Idefics3), accuracy clusters at 47-52% while frontier models (Opus 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-5.4) clear 77-83% and humans reach 84-94%. We use linear probes and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to trace the open-weight failures. Probes of the vision encoder (ViT) and language decoder (LLM) recover Sophon spatial information at 83-85% and 80-86% accuracy, respectively, and the language tower's reasoning circuit answers text-only controls (point clouds as sets of coordinates) at +40 pp over visual stimuli. Language tower per-layer attention attribution localizes visual integration to mid-stack layers. At readout, models elevate the correct answer-set tokens but select among them randomly. We localize the mechanism by tuning individual and grouped components across every architecture's stack, including the vision tower (ViT workspace and reader), the projector, and the language tower (LLM workspace and reader). These experiments demonstrate that this mid-stack workspace holds circuitry capable of organizing the spatial signal so that the frozen reader can decode it. LoRA fine-tuning adjusts that circuitry such that 7B-class VLMs answer Sophon spatial reasoning questions at frontier aggregate accuracy. This work suggests that these models do not have a capability gap for spatial reasoning but suffer from a routing bottleneck that targeted fine-tuning repairs.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As particle-based simulations grow in scale, efficient compression of scientific data becomes critical. Neural point cloud compression (PCC) methods excel on computer vision (CV) benchmarks, but do they transfer to scientific particle data? Supported by an evaluation spanning domains of cosmology, molecular dynamics, and plasma physics, we outline where the structure of scientific data limits current neural approaches and where learning can help in the future.
