185. 【2611.14815】BirdDreams: Joint Perspective and BEV Video Generation for a Plausible, Reactive Driving World Model

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14815

作者：Junyoung Seo,Jaewoo Seo,Seungryong Kim,Laura Leal-Taixé,Xiuming Zhang,Seung Wook Kim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：aim to simulate, Driving world, Driving world models, BEV, world models aim

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/birddreams/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Driving world models aim to simulate how a scene unfolds under different ego actions. This requires plausible motion of surrounding vehicles as well as precise ego-trajectory control. Learning vehicle motion from perspective video alone is challenging because motion is entangled with appearance and vehicles can be occluded. Prior work therefore conditions video generation on layouts that specify vehicle motion, providing control over surrounding vehicles but requiring their future trajectories as inputs. We introduce BirdDreams, an autoregressive driving world model that jointly generates perspective video and a schematic bird's-eye-view (BEV) video, making scene layout part of the predicted future. The BEV, rendered from scene annotations for training, depicts roads and vehicles as simple shapes at a fixed metric scale, providing a top-down view of their positions and motion with reduced occlusion between vehicles. Perspective video, by contrast, retains scene details that the BEV omits, such as traffic signals and object appearance. Joint attention between two video diffusion transformers, one per stream, lets each stream inform the other during generation. The predicted BEV also provides a geometric interface for ego control: we translate and rotate its latest frame along an input ego trajectory and use the resulting frames as a soft condition that shows how the scene would appear to move if only the ego vehicle moved. At inference, BirdDreams generates both videos from a single camera image without requiring an HD map or vehicle-state inputs. On PhysicalAI-AV and nuScenes, BirdDreams follows input ego trajectories more faithfully than prior driving world models and generates more plausible ego motion without ego-action conditioning. Human evaluation shows that BirdDreams outperforms a perspective-only baseline in generating realistic surrounding-vehicle behavior.

186. 【2611.14763】RECALL: Real-Time Recalibration of Multi-Camera Rigs via Learned OEM-Anchored Residuals

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14763

作者：Mario Alberto Tapia de la Cruz,Charalambos Poullis

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：rigs ship pre-calibrated, Multi-camera rigs ship, thermal cycling, continuously through wear, ship pre-calibrated

备注： WACV 2027 submission

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-camera rigs ship pre-calibrated from the manufacturer, but their calibration goes stale each time the rig is repositioned, and drifts continuously through wear, thermal cycling, and vibration. Recovering it on-the-fly is hard: classical bundle adjustment needs a fresh multi-view capture and a global optimization whose cost grows with the rig, ruling out real-time use. In this paper we present RECALL, a feed-forward method that recalibrates a movable multi-camera rig in a single pass. From the nominal OEM calibration as an anchor and a brief observation of a known object, it predicts each camera's current intrinsics and extrinsics, written as a shared global rotation of the rig composed with a small per-camera drift. Instead of regressing the calibration in one shot, RECALL refines its estimate with a learned, rig-coupled recurrent operator that reprojects through a differentiable projection and applies attention across the cameras at each step, and solves for the shared rotational gauge in-network with a learned Gauss-Newton step rather than regressing it. Because the ground truth is synthesizable, we train entirely on data generated on-the-fly and need no captured dataset. On a synthetic 10-camera benchmark, RECALL is more accurate than per-camera and rig-coupled joint bundle adjustment ($0.26^\circ$ vs $0.47^\circ$ rotation, $5.4$ vs $8.9$ px focal) and runs about $10^5\times$ faster at about $0.12$ ms per rig. On real data (TotalCapture, CMU Panoptic) it matches or beats offline rig-coupled bundle adjustment on absolute and relative rotation while running four-to-five orders of magnitude faster, and on the Panoptic surround rig it also matches the strongest image-only foundation models (VGGT, VGGT-$\Omega$, $\pi^3$) on relative pose while staying metric and about $15\times$ more accurate in focal length. Code: this https URL

187. 【2611.14728】SyntheIce: A Synthetic Radargram Annotation Benchmark for Englacial Layer Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14728

作者：Muhammad Behroze Hassan,Bayu Adhi Tama,Sanjay Purushotham,Vandana P. Janeja

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：subsurface ice images, Englacial layers, encode the climate, sheets and glaciers, climate and flow

备注： 10 pages, 10 figures, 3 Tables, accepted in IEEE International Conference on Big Data (BigData) 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Englacial layers in radio-echo-sounding radargrams (subsurface ice images) encode the climate and flow history of ice sheets and glaciers. However, large-scale extraction is hindered by time-consuming manual annotation and semi-automated detectors that produce fragmented rather than continuous horizons. We address two challenges: reconstructing complete layers from partial detections across radargram profiles and validating layer continuity across adjacent tile boundaries. We introduce a slope-aware greedy fragment-linking algorithm that reconstructs full-width layers using row-similarity gating and an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) slope predictor. Each reconstruction receives a content-based hash ID for stable cross-radargram identification. For quantitative evaluation, we generate SyntheIce, a synthetic dataset of 600 radargrams spanning multiple complexity levels and realistic noise conditions, including 150 wide radargrams divided into four adjacent tiles. All samples include exact ground-truth annotations. We evaluate the dataset using XCheck, a consistency-based validation framework, extending its original two-image setting to four consecutive tiles and enforcing continuity at every boundary. This demonstrates the dataset's utility for developing and evaluating real-world annotation algorithms. Our findings extend beyond ice science: when spatial features are fragmented but retain consistent local geometry, trajectory-based linking is more reliable than appearance-based matching. Evaluation against expert-labeled real radargrams demonstrates high precision, recall, F1-score, and mean IoU, supporting the method's transfer to real-world data.

188. 【2611.14686】What Entropy-Based View Selection Cannot See: An Audit of Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14686

作者：Sivasuthan Sukumar,Uthayasanker Thayasivam

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：CLIP can classify, Vision-language models, test images differ, trained to recognize, training data

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Workshops on VLM4RWD

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models such as CLIP can classify images into categories they were never explicitly trained to recognize, but accuracy degrades when test images differ in style or source from their training data. A common remedy generates randomly cropped and flipped copies of each test image, called views, and combines their predictions. Since some views miss the object, methods must identify which views to trust. The dominant approach uses prediction entropy to measure confidence and retains the most certain views. Other methods use signals such as embedding typicality. Despite repeated refinements of these scoring functions, the information they rely on, the model's own response to each view, remains largely unexamined. We investigate this choice while holding other pipeline stages fixed. First, we show that view selection, rather than the gradient update, accounts for almost all accuracy gains. We then examine whether entropy identifies useful views when predictions are wrong, despite at least one view predicting the correct class. In these cases, entropy ranks correct views above incorrect ones no better than chance, recovering only about one-twentieth of the accuracy recovered by an oracle over the same views. This finding holds across two image encoders and under a control ruling out an obvious statistical artifact. An exact analysis explains this limitation: entropy measures confidence but not which class a view favors. We identify an unused signal already available in the augmentation pipeline: the fraction of the original image retained by each crop. Ranking views by this quantity improves accuracy on 14 of 15 benchmark datasets over entropy and on 11 of 15 over the strongest scoring function tested. A pre-method measurement also predicts which datasets benefit.

189. 【2611.14677】Fast Slice-Wise Deep Learning Motion Correction for Spinal Cord Functional MRI

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14677

作者：Thomas Dagonneau,Sandrine Bédard,Nontharat Tucksinapinunchai,Uten Yarach,Loïc Le Folgoc,Pietro Gori,Julien Cohen-Adad

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：MRI time series, functional MRI time, Spinal Cord Toolbox, Respiratory and bulk, functional MRI

备注： 4 pages, 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Respiratory and bulk motion displace the spinal cord between the volumes of a functional MRI time series. Current methods such as the Spinal Cord Toolbox (SCT) take several minutes per time series, and general-purpose brain tools register whole volumes, whereas the cord moves mainly slice by slice. We propose a lightweight convolutional neural network that regresses the in-plane translation aligning each axial slice to a reference. Two models, trained without motion labels, correct large and then residual motion. On a 3T (120 scans) and a 7T (59 scans) dataset, the proposed method improves on SCT on every metric (tSNR +10 to +25%, DVARS -20 to -38%), leaves almost no slice displaced by more than one voxel (0.1%), performs on par with a slice-wise AFNI variant and better than slice-wise FSL FLIRT, and runs in about 10 s per scan, about 30 times faster than SCT. The code is available at this https URL and the method is available in SCT v7.4.

190. 【2611.14608】FreeDeshadow: Shadow Removal for User-Specified Objects With Multimodal Prompts

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14608

作者：Zhiliang Wu,Jihao Gu,Kun Li,Hanyu Xuan,Hehe Fan,Meng Wang,Weisi Lin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：perform global shadow, shadow, shadow removal, global shadow removal, target shadow region

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing shadow removal methods either perform global shadow removal or rely on precise shadow masks to guide restoration. Such paradigms limit their ability to selectively remove the shadow cast by a user-specified object in practical applications. To this end, we introduce an object-aware controllable shadow removal task, which allows users to specify a target object through a simple prompt and removes only its cast shadow while preserving other content. However, object-shadow asymmetry makes it challenging to establish accurate correspondence between the specified object and its spatially separated cast shadow and to confine restoration to the target shadow region. To address these challenges, we propose FreeDeshadow, a unified controllable shadow removal framework that supports both visual and textual prompts. It consists of an object-aware generator and a shadow-aware local refiner. The former interprets the user prompt and produces a high-quality shadow-free proposal, while the latter establishes the correspondence between the object and its cast shadow and constrains the generated changes to the target shadow region. To support training and evaluation, we further construct PCSRD+, a large-scale dataset containing 154,571 quadruples at 1024$\times$1024 resolution and covering six types of user prompts. The visual prompts include a dot, a line, and an object mask, while the textual prompts describe the target object using attributes, spatial relations, or states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeDeshadow accurately removes the shadow of the target object under both visual and textual prompts while effectively preserving non-target regions. It also outperforms existing methods in shadow removal performance and controllability. Project Page: this https URL

191. 【2611.08631】BEVCast: Path-Adaptive BEV for Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08631

作者：Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Zixuan Wang,Weisong Shi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Camera-based driving models, Camera-based driving, build a dense, planning horizon, driving models build

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Camera-based driving models build a dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grid and spend the same computation on every cell of it, although the vehicle crosses only a few of those cells within the planning horizon. We present BEVCast, a two-stage BEV whose resolution adapts to the predicted driving path. A coarse pass encodes the whole scene on a grid of 4.096 m cells. A heat head trained on the recorded path assigns a heat to every coarse cell and the cells with the highest heat are refined into 1.024 m cells under a fixed budget. The remaining cells keep their coarse resolution, so the trajectory head reads a mixed-resolution BEV that still covers the entire scene and needs neither an HD map nor a navigation command. Against a full grid of 1.024 m cells with the same architecture and training recipe, BEVCast at a 10% refinement budget encodes 28% of the BEV queries and needs 66% of the BEV encoder time at batch size 1. On nuScenes its mean displacement error is 1.078 m against 1.149 m and its drivable area violation rate is 1.46% against 2.29%. On NAVSIM navtest its mean displacement error is 0.577 m against 0.598 m and its extended predictive driver model score is 82.51 against 81.28. Next to published planners BEVCast reaches a comparable error on both benchmarks without a navigation command. The resolution of a driving BEV can therefore follow the task instead of the grid.

192. 【2606.21115】MS-rPPG: Multi-spectral State Space Model for Remote Photoplethysmography in Driver Monitoring Systems

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21115

作者：Jiho Choi,Sang Jun Lee

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

关键词：measuring physiological signals, cardiac activity, camera-based technique, technique for measuring, measuring physiological

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) is a camera-based technique for measuring physiological signals, particularly cardiac activity. From the remotely measured signals, heart rate can be estimated, which is crucial for health monitoring. In this study, we investigate a driver health monitoring system based on remote heart rate estimation. However, driving environments represent uncontrolled settings where videos are subject to varying illumination conditions and frequent head movements. We introduce MS-rPPG, a multi-spectral framework that combines RGB with near-infrared (NIR) face video to alleviate rPPG estimation under challenging driving conditions. To combine the complementary features from two spectral videos, we propose a cross-spectral linear modulation (CSLM) strategy based on frequency-domain analysis. Moreover, we introduce MS-Mamba, a novel state space model designed to effectively model long-range temporal dependencies while jointly capturing cross-channel interactions between multi-spectral features. We collected a real-world dataset called MS-Drive, which was recorded from 50 participants while driving the vehicle. The proposed method was evaluated on the MR-NIRP Car dataset and MS-Drive datasets. The experimental results indicate that MS-rPPG shows better robustness and heart rate estimation accuracy than previous methods, highlighting its promise for driver health monitoring. The codes are available at this http URL.

193. 【2611.17111】Spatiotemporal learning improves quantification of pore-space evolution and fluid displacement

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17111

作者：Zhuangzhuang Ma,Rukuan Chai,Qianqian Ma,Yanghua Wang,Branko Bijeljic,Martin J. Blunt

类目：Fluid Dynamics (physics.flu-dyn); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：underground hydrogen storage, geological carbon storage, affecting geological carbon, groundwater contaminant transport, carbon storage

备注： 48 pages, 9 figures, 18 tables, including Supplementary Information

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pore-space evolution and fluid displacement influence how rocks transmit and store fluids, affecting geological carbon storage, underground hydrogen storage and groundwater contaminant transport. A single time-resolved micro-CT experiment may produce tens to hundreds of three-dimensional images, and segmentation is required to quantify physical properties and run image-based simulations. Traditional methods such as thresholding perform poorly on noisy, low-contrast images and need manual adjustment per image; 3D deep learning networks segment each image without temporal information, so their errors vary between images and could be mistaken for physical change. Here we introduce a transformer-based 4D segmentation framework combining spatial and temporal information. It uses sequential images to resolve ambiguous phase assignments and separate physical change from segmentation error. The method is tested on images of mineral dissolution: it measures pore-volume growth and rock boundaries more accurately than an equivalent 3D model that ignores temporal information. It also captures the increase in permeability verified against reference segmentations and flow simulations. The advantage transfers to another dissolution sample without retraining. On two other datasets of fluid displacement, it reduces spurious changes in predicted rock and porosity fluctuations and accurately identifies filling events. Spatiotemporal segmentation turns sequences of hundreds of three-dimensional images into more reliable quantitative measurements of pore-space evolution and fluid displacement without per-image parameter adjustment to support the analysis of time-resolved CT imaging.

194. 【2611.15758】Physics-based simulation unlocks scaling laws for generalizable MRI reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15758

作者：Hong Shang,Zijian Zhan,Sen Jia,Xiang Fan,Jintao Meng,Yang Zhou,Xiang Zhou,Tianyu He,Xinran Chen,Qiaoling Zeng,Keyan Yu,Jianhua Yao,Dong Liang

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：holds great promise, translation remains bottlenecked, clinical translation remains, magnetic resonance imaging, reconstruction holds great

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep-learning-based magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction holds great promise for accelerating acquisitions, yet its clinical translation remains bottlenecked by poor generalizability across unseen scanners, populations, anatomies, and protocols. Gathering sufficiently diverse clinical datasets to overcome this bottleneck is hindered by high acquisition costs, privacy restrictions, and institutional biases. Here, we introduce Scalable Inverse Model (SIM), a framework that systematically co-designs physics-simulated training data, model architecture, and learning paradigms around scalability--leveraging compute scaling to overcome historical data bottlenecks and achieve robust zero-shot MRI reconstruction. Across 14 public and in-house datasets--spanning seven international centers, five vendors, field strengths of 0.3-7 T, over ten anatomical regions, more than ten acquisition protocols, downstream quantitative and molecular tasks, and a 150-scan blinded radiologist study--SIM demonstrates robust zero-shot generalizability, recovering fine anatomical details while suppressing aliasing artifacts where conventional deep-learning methods fail. Attention-map analyses confirm that SIM learns physically explainable, dynamically adaptive reconstruction primitives rather than anatomy-specific priors. SIM establishes a scalable, privacy-preserving, and democratized infrastructure for universal MRI reconstruction, providing a blueprint for generalizable AI across inverse problems in medical imaging.

195. 【2611.15370】Supervision-Aware Evaluation of MedGemma for Reference-Assisted Radiotherapy Contour Ranking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15370

作者：Jaden Moon,Caiwen Jiang,Songlin Zhao,Wei Liu

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Radiotherapy contour ranking, support quality assurance, Radiotherapy contour, score construction, quality assurance

备注： 5 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables. Submitted to IEEE ISBI 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Radiotherapy contour ranking can support quality assurance, but vision-language model (VLM) gains may reflect supervision, score construction, available inputs, or representation access. We evaluated frozen MedGemma 1.5 in a single-institution study, a 12-patient held-out HaN-Seg test, five-fold cross-fitting on 30 non-test HaN-Seg patients, and transfer without refitting to 120 SegRap2023 patients. On held-out HaN-Seg, a supervised readout of the final prompt state exceeded a supervised readout of the model's yes/no output probabilities by +0.3047 pooled average precision (AP; 95% patient-bootstrap interval [+0.2822, +0.3269]); the advantage persisted against a parameter-count-matched nonlinear control. A readout of projected visual tokens exceeded the final-state readout in cross-fitting and transfer (+0.0706 AP averaged equally across patients on SegRap; 95% interval [+0.0607, +0.0804]), while a structured panel-feature readout remained competitive with visual tokens. The anatomy-conditioned readout did not demonstrate an advantage over the native-feature comparator. These results motivate visual-token and structured-feature controls in medical-imaging VLM evaluations; outcomes were computational or synthetic, not clinical acceptability.

196. 【2611.14979】Coarse-to-Fine 3D MRI Reconstruction via Resolution-Agnostic Neural Operators

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14979

作者：Jiayun Wang,Ram Daftari,Weijie Gan,Valentin Duruisseaux,Ruibo Wang,Armeet Singh Jatyani,Boris Bonev,Anima Anandkumar

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：GPU memory, ignores through-plane, MRI reconstruction, limited in practice, memory and compute

备注： NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep-learning-based 3D MRI reconstruction is limited in practice by the GPU memory and compute cost of full-volume training; 3D acquisitions are thus most often reconstructed by 2D models applied slice-by-slice and then stacked, which ignores through-plane (stacking-axis) structure. We reconstruct in full 3D and ask whether a model can be trained at low resolution (fast and memory-feasible) yet deployed at higher resolution. This coarse-to-fine strategy needs reliable cross-resolution performance, but standard networks are resolution-specific and degrade substantially when evaluated outside the training setting. We therefore use resolution-agnostic neural operators (NOs) and propose KIR-NO (k-space-to-Image Reconstruction Neural Operator), a discretization-agnostic framework for accelerated 3D MRI reconstruction. KIR-NO is trained with full backpropagation on low-resolution volumes and applied zero-shot to higher-resolution volumes at inference. Its core building block is a 3D neural operator based on discrete-continuous convolutions: convolutional filters are parameterized in a continuous basis and sampled at arbitrary voxel resolutions while preserving the local inductive bias of convolution. KIR-NO integrates these learned operators with physics-based consistency: a k-space neural-operator head first refines the undersampled Fourier measurements, and image-space neural-operator cascades then refine the reconstruction through interleaved data-consistency updates. Across 3D knee and brain MRI benchmarks, KIR-NO improves over classical and CNN baselines: it improves over a state-of-the-art 3D CNN by 2.2 dB PSNR at half resolution under 4x acceleration on SKM-TEA, with gains persisting at 8x and 16x. These results show that resolution-agnostic operators make coarse-to-fine 3D MRI reconstruction - cheap low-resolution training with high-resolution deployment - practical across anatomies.

197. 【2611.14975】Aggregate Accuracy Hides Diagnosis-Specific Failure in Medical Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14975

作者：Shreya Ajay Kale,Angad Ripudaman Singh Bajwa

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：hide complete failure, Medical vision-language models, open medical VLMs, single diagnosis, hide complete

备注： Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Medical Reasoning with Vision Language Foundation Models (Med-Reasoner). 16 pages including NeurIPS checklist, 1 figure, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are compared mainly by accuracy averaged across findings, a summary that can hide complete failure on a single diagnosis. We audit five open medical VLMs (MedGemma-4B, Lingshu-7B, HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MedVLM-R1) on 6,048 ChestX-ray14 questions balanced by sex, age, finding, and label. On the 432 pneumothorax-positive X-rays, HuatuoGPT-Vision and Qwen2.5-VL detect zero cases while MedGemma and Lingshu detect 38% and 67% of the same images. The two failing models answer "no" to all 864 pneumothorax cases (balanced accuracy 0.500, chance), yet produce fluent, anatomically specific explanations that name the signs of a collapsed lung and then deny them, with no hedge or abstention. This failure is not an artifact of prompting, image resolution, label ambiguity, or model scale. It persists across four prompt formats, at native resolution, and on single-label cases. A within-model control shows the same forced prompt gives HuatuoGPT 79% recall on nodule but only 6% on pneumothorax. It survives a tenfold scale increase (Qwen2.5-VL-72B detects 4%) and replicates on SIIM-ACR, an independent dataset with radiologist-drawn masks, where the failing models detect 0 of 432 confirmed pneumothoraces, including 0 of the 144 largest lesions. Reading the models' logits and internal states shows this is not blindness. The two failing models are highly confident yet no better than chance and a linear probe recovers pneumothorax from their hidden representations even where recall is zero, so the finding is represented internally but suppressed at the output. Aggregate accuracy therefore cannot establish that a medical VLM can see a given finding, because a model that misses one can still describe it convincingly. Reporting per-finding recall and specificity on balanced cases is needed to tell the difference.

198. 【2611.14947】One Block Is Enough: LoopMR for Accelerated MRI Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14947

作者：Yimeng He,Yibin Xie,Yi Zheng,Yifan Gao,Lixia Wang,Yin-Chen Hsu,Yufeng Wang,Meng Lu,Debiao Li,Xiuzhen Huang

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Deep-learning unrolled networks, Deep-learning unrolled, unrolled networks, leading approach, approach to accelerated

备注： 10 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/hym97/LoopMR)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep-learning unrolled networks are the leading approach to accelerated MRI reconstruction. State-of-the-art models such as PromptMR+ learn a distinct block for every cascade, so their size grows with the number of cascades. Sharing one block across iterations avoids adding parameters, but earlier shared-weight models typically used a tiny block and produced worse images than models with distinct weights. Therefore, it remained unclear whether weight sharing itself or the choice of block was the problem. Recent advances in looped transformers, which reuse the same block across layers, show that they can match or outperform their distinct-weight counterparts on several tasks. Motivated by this, we propose LoopMR, which reuses a single Restormer block at every iteration as its core. Besides the current image estimate, the block receives data-consistency inputs and latent images. On four datasets covering the brain (CC359), knee (fastMRI), heart (CMRxRecon2023) and carotid (in-house), LoopMR significantly outperforms E2E-VarNet, PromptMR and PromptMR+ in PSNR and SSIM at every acceleration factor (p 0.01), with about 1/9 of PromptMR+'s parameters (10.3M versus 92.9M). It also keeps its lead at unseen acceleration factors and on an unseen dataset. With our transformer block, the shared model matches or outperforms its distinct-weight counterpart at 6, 12 and 24 iterations. At a fixed number of iterations, shared transformer blocks match or outperform their distinct-weight versions, while shared CNN blocks perform worse. These results suggest that looping one well-chosen block can outperform stacking distinct cascades in MRI reconstruction, with a fraction of the parameters.
